# Sketch 011 — the recipe route, layout C

**Status:** SETTLED — layout C (Mark, 2026-09-24); width ladder derived and approved (Mark, 2026-09-26, decision 16). Acceptance target for Phase 03.5; built in plans 03.5-10 to 03.5-18 (decisions 15, 16, 18 and 19), conformance in 03.5-LADDER-CONFORMANCE.md.
**Drawn:** 2026-09-23 to 2026-09-24.
**Question:** where recipe context, the Sheet and the batch log sit, from desktop to phone.

## Why this sketch exists

Phase 03.5 separates the recipe record from the Sheet (`.impeccable/surfaces/route-recipe.md` § "03.5 revision"); § 4 of that section says the chosen layout is drawn at 1366, 1024 and 393 on the Sprinkles canvas and snapshotted here as the acceptance target. 03.5 plans lock against this folder. It is numbered 011 because `010-bench-sheet` is reserved for the Phase 4 bench sheet (`04-CONTEXT.md` D-02).

## The ladder

Derived 2026-09-26 from measured content limits (decision 16), not picked. Four layouts; each cut is a sum of set values.

| Window | Nav | Sheet | Log | Table, margin, band | Boards |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1590 and up | rail (224), pinned under the sticky header; the header has no menu button | two columns (Sheet 920 at 1590, 930 at 1600, 1100 from 1770) | beside, 350 | D3 table, 48, band side by side | 1920, 1600, 1600-sticky-rail |
| 1366 to 1589 | sticky header with a menu button; the rail is the fly-out | two columns (Sheet 920 at 1366, 1100 from 1546 to 1589) | beside, 350 | D3 table, 48, band side by side | 1366 |
| 984 to 1365 | sticky header and fly-out | two columns (Sheet 920 at 984) | below | D3 table, 48, band side by side, Go to batch row | 1024, 984 |
| 724 to 983 | sticky header and fly-out (Rule A, Mark 2026-10-03) | one column | below | D3 table, 48, band side by side, Go to batch row | 983, 834, 744 |
| below 724 | bottom tab row and the scrolling header | one column | below | list table, 20, band stacked | 723, 393 |

From a 1770 window the content stops growing at 1482 (Sheet 1100 + 32 + log 350) and centres in the main area. **Amended 2026-10-03 (decision 33, Mark): the ladder above replaces the one drawn on 2026-09-26, whose rail was in every row from 984. The cuts are 1590 (the rail returns: 224 + 3 x 32 + 350 + 920), 1366 (the log beside), 984 (the Sheet in two columns) and 724 (the sticky header and the fly-out; the tab row and the scrolling header below).**

The 393 board was verified on Mark's iPhone over the LAN; the 393 board forces the app's narrow media rules because the canvas gives an artboard no narrow viewport of its own.

## Decisions

Taken in substance from `.planning/.continue-here.md` `<decisions_made>` and from `route-recipe.md` § "03.5 revision" parts 2 and 7, attributed to Mark with dates.

1. Layout C — recipe and version as App front matter across the top, History rail beneath, the log beside the Sheet at desktop and 1366, below it at 1024 and 393.
2. Sheet table style 6, and the hand (Caveat, pen blue, 20px minimum) for every batch edit on the Sheet — as-made grams and Instructions changes; this bends DESIGN.md's Hand Rule and the rule change is recorded in the 03.5 discussion.
3. The Source column becomes an "estimated" chip after the name, ink, target-chip outline.
4. History is a dated rail, versions only, oldest left, opening scrolled to the version in view, Notebook red filled/hollow/ring.
5. The pen opens from Next version with the ceremony in the version column of the band, steps edit one at a time via "edit this step", Done keeps the edit in the draft and Cancel restores the parent's step, remove sits on a closed step, only the ceremony saves — superseding the version brief's "the pen keeps the page" paragraph for steps. On the open step, Done is a hairline ink box and Cancel an underlined word, Cancel first (Sheet grammar; Mark, 2026-09-24). The step's uses control reads "change the ingredients", not "change" (Mark, 2026-09-24); the built app's "change" follows in 03.5.
6. Below desktop the folds are version Details, Balance with Things to check, and Tasting, closed by default and closed again on every visit with no stored state, while Ingredients, Instructions, Before you start and the churn cells stay open, and the Sheet reads whole at desktop and in print.
7. The control beside the Recipe name reads "Rename".
8. The destination caption is dropped from recipe views and the rail mark stays.
9. The Recipe name "Olive Oil Ice Cream, circulator" and the Sheet title "Olive Oil Ice Cream" are drawn to show the two names apart.
10. Carried forward notes are dropped: no block on the Sheet, no fold below desktop, "Notes" leaves the Sheet's parts list, the three seeded notes' words are dropped rather than moved, the inherited-note marker stays on Before you start notes, and removing carriedForward from code, seed, store and transfer validation is 03.5 scope.
11. Instructions names the steps region on screen and in print — screen-only, code identifiers keep method.
12. The Recipe Book form gets authored Yield and time fields as Sheet fields on the version, which time fields and how they sit beside the derived Makes, Age, Harden and Serve being settled when the form is built, and the form stays unbuilt in 03.5.
13. The Recipe Book form's footer names the Recipe name with its qualifier while the Sheet title stays the heading, and its running head is gone.
14. Phone logging is deferred out of 03.5 to the brief for phone-based jobs (`.planning/todos/pending/2026-09-24-write-a-product-brief-for-phone-based-jobs.md`), so the 393 board settles layout, not whether the log is entered on the phone. Mark chose option C on 2026-10-02 (the phone transcribes); decision 30 draws it.
15. The ingredient table's amount and name are two columns under one "Ingredient" head, at every width (Mark, 2026-09-25/26): the table sizes to content, so the amount, As made and % of batch take their figures' width (the numeric heads may wrap) and the name takes the rest, wrapping inside its own column; the "estimated" tag and the portion line stay with the name. At 393 the plan amount, the name and the share sit on line one, and as made sits under the plan amount on the same right edge. The side nav shows only from 1024 up (Mark, 2026-09-25) — superseded by decision 16, which puts the side nav's cut at 984.
16. The width ladder is derived from measured content limits (Mark, 2026-09-26; the study is https://claude.ai/artifact/2VNpa4tVnp2hh6E7iKYmnW). Set values: the Sheet's two-column minimum is 696 (as on the 1366 board; 660 was drawn and rejected as too bunched), the log's minimum and width beside the Sheet is 350, gutters are 32 (page margin, Sheet to log, page margin), and the content maximum is 1482 (the two-column Sheet stops gaining at about 1100), centred in the main area. Precedence: the log moves below the Sheet first; then the side nav and the Sheet's second column go together; the list-form ingredient table, the 20px page margin and the stacked band go together below 724 (moved up to 984 and rejected for its white space). So: log beside from 1366 (224 + 96 + 696 + 350), side nav and two-column Sheet from 984 (224 + 64 + 696), phone forms below 724 (band 660 + 64). Touch sizes follow the pointer only, no longer a width. The History rail's nodes sit on top of its line (Mark, 2026-09-26).
17. The Why is typed in the prose-field role and shown in the hand once saved (Mark, 2026-09-27: "type in the prose and show in the hand"). On 1600-pen.html the Why textarea is drawn in the text face at 16px, leading 1.5, pen blue, like every other entry field; the saved Why in the version details and History stays in the hand. The board previously drew the textarea in Caveat at 22px, the only entry field in the hand (canvas version 244 corrects it).
18. The folds are a convenience at every width, open by default from 1366 and closed by default below it (Mark, 2026-09-27, after the desktop-state study https://claude.ai/artifact/J7LiG5oEA8YeNjzH2Ugvbw). Set value: the open Balance column's minimum is 190, so the open state fits from the log's cut, 1366 = 224 + 3 × 32 + 696 + 350. Folding serves two purposes, "shortening the page, and hiding info that is not of interest at the moment", and is most critical at phone width. The fold controls exist at every width, with no stored state. Balance and Things to check fold separately, each with its own control. "Things to check" becomes "Watch for", and the "derived" label is dropped (Mark, 2026-09-27). The band's rhythm moves with the folds' default: roomy from 1366, tight below. This supersedes decision 6's "the Sheet reads whole at desktop" on screen; print still reads whole. Drawn on canvas version 247: every C board from 1366 up draws the folds open with a Hide control ("Hide details", and "Hide" beside the Balance, Watch for and Tasting heads); every board below 1366 draws them closed with Show. The 1366 board takes the roomy band.
19. Counts and the upright rails (Mark, 2026-09-27, after the Impeccable critique of the count options in `.planning/sketches/011-options-counts/`). **One version:** History stays in the band as one plain line, "Only this version so far", with no rail and no fold control. **Many versions:** History folds like the other sections, its Show/Hide beside the label, open by default from 1366 and closed below (decision 18); from 1366 it is the horizontal rail in the band, and below 1366 it stands upright, latest first, one row per version with its written and churned dates. Closed, it shows its label, Show and the count ("2 versions"). **No batches:** the log offers Record a batch and Print sheet, unchanged. **One batch:** no Batches control; the head carries Correct and Record another. **Many batches:** option D, the batches of the version in view stand as an upright rail above the batch in view, latest first, at every width (the 350 column from 1366, under the Sheet below): filled where tasted, hollow where not yet tasted, the one in view ringed, each row with its tasting and out-of-machine reading. The batch list folds like the other sections, its Show/Hide beside the "Batches" label, open by default from 1366 and closed below; closed, it shows the label, Show and the count, and the batch in view follows (Mark, 2026-09-27). After the 393 critique (Mark, 2026-09-27, "all of them"): the History and Batches heads are one control each, the whole row a 44px-tall button holding the label, Show or Hide, and the count, styled as the app's text control; the batch count is in words ("3 batches"); a version never churned says "not churned" on its row; a saved draft above the version in view is named in the band under it ("Draft: Version 2 · less oil"), so "Latest" is not read as the newest row; every rail link carries tabindex 0. Every fold head takes the same full-row control (Mark, 2026-09-27): Version ("Show details"), History, Balance, Watch for, Tasting and Batches each make their whole head row one 44px-tall button, the label then Show or Hide, and any count or date at the row's end; Tasting's date moves to the end, so its Show sits straight after the label. Balance and Watch for keep their h2, with the button inside it. The phone's reading order and its filled action stay as drawn until the phone is shaped. The main boards draw one batch, so only the count boards show the batch rail; the reference drawings are `batches-many.html` (D) and `upright-393.html`.
20. Tasting is its own section (Mark, UAT 2026-09-30, item 9). In the batch log it is a `section` named Tasting whose heading is an `h2`, the same level as Balance and Watch for, holding the fold button (the closed and open boards) or the label and tasted date (the count boards). It stays where it sits, nested in the Batch section after the churn cells and notes and before Next time and the Recorded line; only its element and heading level change, and nothing is drawn differently (the h2 resets the browser's heading margin and inherits the face). Moving it out as a sibling after the Batch section would put the Recorded line before Tasting, which no board draws and Mark did not ask for; that reading is Mark's to choose. The record pen's own Tasting head is not drawn on any board and is not part of this decision.
21. The rails' line connects nodes only (Mark, UAT 2026-09-30, "the gray line draws over the circles", and his 2026-09-29 decision that the horizontal rail connects nodes too). **Upright** (History below 1366, the batch list at every width): the line runs from 2px below one mark to the top of the next row and never crosses, pokes above or stops short of a circle; `upright-393.html` already drew it so, and the app now matches it. **Horizontal** (History from 1366): the hairline runs from the first node's centre to the last node's centre, not across the whole rail. The generator draws the track and the node row inside one strip as wide as its content, the track's left the row's padding plus half a mark (12) and its right the padding plus a node's width less half a mark (168 from the strip's right). Node positions, the fade and the overflow do not move. A hollow mark still fills with the app background, so a line meeting it is hidden. The line paints beneath the marks, by tree order and positioning, with no z-index.
22. Fold-head counts carry only the count (Mark, UAT 2026-09-30, G-03.5-R2-1: some of the Claude Design notes ended up in the UX). The History and Batches heads read "2 versions" and "3 batches" (and "1 version", "8 versions"), open or closed, at every width. The reading-order hints the generators drew into open heads (" · latest first", " · oldest left, latest right", " · opens at the version in view") are gone from every board, and the app's copy of them goes with them. Row order stays described only as a description (decision 19's rows, the rail's own order), never as head text. The unpicked batch timeline option's legend and caption lost "oldest left" too (Mark, 2026-10-01), so its head reads "3 · hollow: not yet tasted"; `batches-many.html` and `batches-many-393.html` in `011-options-counts` were regenerated for it. The reference board `recipe-book-form-reference.html` was checked against a fresh snapshot of its generated source and differs in far more than the History head's count text (its band, version block and rail markup), so it is left unchanged, hint included, until the Recipe Book form is built.
23. The boards' touch floor reads the pointer alone (Mark, UAT 2026-09-30, G-03.5-R2-3, option 2). The boards' stylesheet carried the app's old width arm, so a fine-pointer window at 723 drew 44px controls the app does not (Rename 44 on the board, 17 in the app). `snapshot.py` now narrows that condition to `(pointer:coarse)` in the copy it inlines, so decision 16's "touch sizes follow the pointer only" holds on every board; the 393 board's forced rules are unchanged, since it is read with a coarse pointer. The canvas's own stylesheet asset carried the old condition too; on 2026-10-01 it was replaced by `/_blob/8adf6a1ce6e9505b8dc487280a2ce3d7` (the same stylesheet with only that media condition narrowed), and `gen.py`, `qty.py` and `snapshot.py` point at it.
24. **Approved by Mark (2026-10-02; Sid proposed it the same day):** Show changes on the ingredient table in the list form, below 724, draws the struck old figure above the current one, right-aligned in the same tracks, in both the amount and the % of batch (option 1b). Found missing from every board: the struck and current amounts side by side are 83 to 104px wide in a 64px track and overlap the name (Mexican Chocolate v4: 13 of 13 rows, up to 30px; the Total row on Mocha v3, Coconut v2 and Underbelly v2; every width 320 to 723, WebKit). Stacking both columns leaves the name track at its reading-view width (223px at 393, 150px at 320) so nothing in the name column moves when Show changes turns on; each changed row grows one line (38px to 56px at 393). A batch in view puts the as-made hand under the current amount, three lines. A removed row draws one struck amount, like its struck share, where the app today also prints the unchanged number after it. Options 1 (amount only), 2 (wider track) and 3 (struck figure under the name) are drawn on `011-options-show-changes/list-form-options.html`. Mark's four answers, 2026-10-02 (his words: "1b, fix the removed row, include the pen, leave the Total"): (1) option 1b, the struck old figure above the new one, right-aligned, in both the amount and the % of batch tracks; (2) the removed row is approved as drawn on `393-show-changes-cases.html`, one struck amount on one line with no unchanged number after it, which needs a change to `DiffGramsCell` in the app (the fix task does it); (3) the pen is in the same fix, drawn first as decision 25; (4) the Total row stays as drawn, the struck total without a unit, stacked, right-aligned at the number, and no unit is added. **Amended 2026-10-03 by decision 31 (Mark chose D3: "D3 is my favorite. iPhone follows the design too. Plan / as made / struck. I want keep things static when Show changes toggles."; the boards below are redrawn and drawn, awaiting Mark's look, since Mark has chosen the design but has not seen them):** below 724 the cell order in the amount, the % of batch and the Total is the plan amount, then As made, then the struck old figure, not the struck figure above the new one. Answers (1) and the struck-above position they describe are superseded; (2) the removed row (one struck amount, now on the first line, where a plan amount stands) and (4) the Total (struck total without a unit, now last) stand as drawn; the 64px amount track and the name track at its reading-view width (223 at 393, 150 at 320) are unchanged. The boards `393-show-changes`, `723-show-changes` and `393-show-changes-cases` are redrawn; `011-options-show-changes/list-form-options.html` stays as the 2026-10-02 history (option 1b).

25. **Approved by Mark (2026-10-02, "looks good what I saw in the sketch"; Sid drew it the same day):** the pen's changed row at phone widths. Mark said to include the pen in the Show changes fix (decision 24, answer 3) and has since looked at the boards and approved them as drawn. Below 724 the struck parent amount stacks above the grams field, right-aligned in the 64px amount track, the same two declarations as decision 24 on one more selector (the pen's struck parent is a direct child of the amount cell, not of the plan-grams slot). The field stays 52px wide (44px tall on a coarse pointer) and clear of the name. A row whose share only moved stacks its share and keeps the field's height (63px at 393 coarse), and a row with no change stays one line. The Total stacks its struck figure with no unit, as decision 24 draws it. Drawn on `393-pen-changes.html` and `723-pen-changes.html` from the built app's own markup (Mexican Chocolate v4 opened from Next version: Whole Milk 563 to 600, Sucrose 33.4 to 36, Cocoa Powder 40.8 to 45, Cinnamon removed, every other amount as seeded). Measured in the app with the selector applied, widths 320 to 723 (404 widths), Playwright WebKit and Chromium, coarse and fine pointer: struck-and-field-and-share collisions 0 at every width (today 404 of 404, up to 24.8px WebKit and 22px Chromium), narrowest name track 150px at 320 (103px today), page overflow 0, field 52 x 44 coarse and 52 x 26.3 fine, all 60 corner and centre hit points of the 12 coarse fields land on the field at 320, 393 and 723. The boards match the app cell for cell (rows, amount, name, share, field: 0 mismatches at 393 and 723, both engines). Device-unverified: Mark's iPad and iPhone were not used, and neither Playwright engine reproduces the device's text rendering. **Amended 2026-10-03 by decision 31 (Mark chose D3, "iPhone follows the design too"; `393-pen-changes` and `723-pen-changes` redrawn, drawn, awaiting Mark's look):** the pen's struck parent stands under the grams field, on the row grid's last line, not above it, so the field stays on the plan amount's line and opening the pen moves nothing. The field stays 52px wide (44px tall on a coarse pointer); the Total puts its struck figure under the current one without a unit; a removed row keeps its field first and the struck parent under it. The share's struck figure stands on the same last line as the amount's.

26. **Approved by Mark (2026-10-02, "14px, fix it"; Sid proposed it the same day):** the pen's remove and restore links stand clear of the name and the estimated tag. Mark's words (iPhone): "in Correct or Next version mode on iPhone, (when the remove link is visible), the remove link doesn't have enough white space to separate it from either ingredient name or estimated tag". **What the app does:** `RemoveRowControl` prints its button straight after the name, the estimated tag and any orphaned-row flag with nothing between, so the underlined word touches them: 0px on every width 320 to 723 on Mexican Chocolate v4 (12 rows with remove), Mocha v3 (14) and Strawberry v2.1 (11), Playwright WebKit and system Chrome, coarse and fine pointer ("Sucroseremove", "[estimated]remove"). Where name plus tag is long enough the link wraps onto its own line, flush under the name (3 of 12 rows at 393), and reads fine. **Correct:** the batch amend pen (the "Correct" word on the batch head) is the recording pen; its ingredient table renders no remove or restore control at all (measured: 0 on Mexican Chocolate v3 after Correct opens; its only "remove"-like control is the Method step's "done differently"). `RemoveRowControl` is rendered once, in the Next version pen, so the defect is in Next version alone. **What the wide authority draws:** `1600-pen.html` prints a word space and `margin-left:10px`, which measures 14px from the last mark of the name or the tag to the link in WebKit, 14.2px in Chrome. The app never carried either. **The wrapped case:** a plain `margin-left` also applies at the start of a wrapped line, so a link that now sits flush under the name would indent 10px (16px in the table form, which has 6px of cell padding). A margin on the preceding content cannot reach the name, which is a bare text node; putting the cell in flex or grid would turn the name and the tag into separate items and change how a long name and its tag wrap (decision 24's long-name case), so it was not drawn. **Recommended (drawn):** the gap is a collapsible word space with extra word spacing, in its own span before the button: `<span class="ingredient-table__remove-gap"> </span>`, `word-spacing: var(--sheet-remove-gap)`, token `--sheet-remove-gap: 10px` in the `--sheet-*` family beside `--sheet-flag-gap` (the 10px is added to the word space, 4px at these sizes, so the link stands 14px from the last mark, as on the wide board). A collapsible space is dropped at the start of a line, so a wrapped link stays flush at 0 (6 in the table form, as today) and a link that stays on the line has the full gap. The button is untouched: 44.7 x 44 coarse (WebKit; 43.1 x 44 Chrome), x 24 fine, the same as today. **Measured on the real DOM with the rule injected in-page** (the :4173 build, no app edit), widths 320 to 723, 404 widths, three recipes, WebKit and Chrome, coarse and fine: same-line gap 14 (WebKit) and 14.2 (Chrome) at every width and row, none under 4px (today none over 0); wrapped-link left offset 0 (a margin of 10 gives 10); name track, share track and amount track moved at 0 row-widths; page overflow 0; from 724 to 1700 (fine pointer, table form) the gap is 14 and the % column moves at most 0.1px (sub-pixel). The restore word (Cinnamon removed) and the orphaned-row state (a step removed, every row it used flagged, the row's remove link on its own line under the flag) measured the same: restore 14px after the struck name, orphan rows flush at 0. Board against app, side by side, 393 and 723, both engines: 14 rows, rows, amount, name, share, field and link rectangles, 0 mismatches. **What the wrapped-case answer costs:** the gap is 14px of width the name line did not have, so rows near the limit wrap that did not. At 393 on Mexican Chocolate v4 two more rows wrap (Cocoa Powder, Vanilla Extract: 3 of 12 today, 5 of 12), each 18px taller (WebKit coarse; 15px fine), 36px more in the table. Over all 404 widths 130 of 4,848 row-widths on Mexican Chocolate v4 (2.7%), 130 of 5,656 on Mocha v3 (2.3%) and 102 of 4,444 on Strawberry v2.1 (2.3%) grow by one line in WebKit; Chrome 127, 127 and 99. A 10px margin instead would wrap fewer (90, 90 and 70 row-widths) but the link would sit 10px from the last mark, short of the wide board's 14, and indent on every wrapped row. The remove link's right end stays inside the name track, so it keeps at least the grid's 10px column gap to the share. Drawn on `393-pen-changes.html` and `723-pen-changes.html` (the generator adds the span to the app's captured markup, `showchanges.py` `with_remove_gap`); `1600-pen.html` already draws the gap and wraps nowhere, so it is unchanged. **Device-unverified:** Mark's iPhone and iPad were not used; the gap is a word space, so the iPhone's own font metrics set the 4px. **For Mark:** the 14px (or a smaller value), and whether a 10px indent on a wrapped link would be acceptable instead of the one extra span (the app's chip already indents 8px when it wraps). **Amended 2026-10-03 (Mark, looking at the final `1600-pen` board: "the remove link on Whole milk (shared 12g row) is below all the other lines instead of being next to estimated"):** a split row's remove link belongs on the name's line, next to the name and the estimated tag, with the portion line ("120 g of 370.4 g · 46.3% in all") under both. The app, and the board of 2026-09-24 it was drawn from, put the link after the portion line, which is a block, so the link fell under it at every width (see the addendum "The split row's remove link" in decision 33). Proposed, drawn on `1600-pen` and `1600-remove-link`; not approved.

27. **Approved by Mark (2026-10-02, "sticky nav is good"; Sid drew it the same day):** the side nav's options stay on screen while the page scrolls (Mark, 2026-09-27: "make the side-nav sticky so that when shown, the nav options don't scroll off the screen"). **What the app does today:** at 984 and up the rail is a flex column that stretches to the page's full height (3,411px at 984, 3,016px at 1366, 2,714px at 1920 on Olive Oil v1), and its options sit at the top of it, so at 1,500px of scroll the first option is at y -1,426 and every option is off the screen. **Drawn:** one wrapper element inside the rail, `.shell__rail-inner`, carries the options and is `position: sticky; top: var(--gap-xs)`. The rail itself stays as it is, full page height with its right hairline, padding and 224px width, so the divider still runs the whole page at every scroll position and nothing else moves. `top` is the rail's own top padding (6px), so a pinned nav sits 6px under the window's top as it sits 6px under the head at the top of the page. The head scrolls away; only the options are pinned. Drawn on `1366-sticky-nav.html` (the window 640px down, the log beside the Sheet) and `984-sticky-nav.html` (2,800px down, the log below): each board is the window, the page shifted up inside it, and the rule is real CSS on the board. **Measured in the built app** (WebKit and Chrome, Olive Oil v1, the wrapper and the rule injected in-page, no app edit), windows 984 x 768, 1366 x 1024 and 1920 x 1080, at the top, 1,500px down and at the end of the page: the options at y 6 to 296 at 1,500px and at the end (74 to 364 at the top, as today), the rail 224 wide, the log's x and width unchanged (256 and 696 at 984, 984 and 350 at 1366, 1463 and 350 at 1920), the page's scroll width equal to the window's (no sideways scroll), the page height unchanged. At 984 x 300 the nav stays whole until the last 22px of the page, where the rail's own 32px bottom padding lifts it; shorter windows are not supported. No overflow rule is drawn: the nav is 290px tall, so a window of 330px or more shows all of it, and `overflow-y: auto` on the wrapper would clip the focus ring on the places at the wrapper's edges (the ring sits 2px outside the place, and the places fill the wrapper). **Not drawn, and why:** making the rail itself sticky with `align-self: flex-start` needs no new element, but shortens the rail to 328px (measured at 1366, WebKit and Chrome), so the hairline would end under Kitchen instead of running the page; keeping the full-height hairline then needs it moved to the routed page's left edge, which takes the hairline's 1px from the routed page's width, so the rail would narrow by 1px to keep decision 16's sums (984 = 224 + 2 x 32 + 696). **Device-unverified:** Mark's iPad was not used; the engines are Playwright WebKit and system Chrome. **For Mark:** whether a pinned nav should keep the 6px gap under the window's top, and whether a nav that grows past a short window's height should ever scroll itself (not needed at today's five places).

28. **Approved by Mark (2026-10-02, "move the pen cut to 724"; Sid measured and proposed it the same day, and drew the pen at 724, 740 and 759 for the look):** the record pen's own cut, derived from its measured limits (todo 2026-09-26). Mark's 2026-09-27 checkpoint kept the cut at 760 until the pen was measured; this is the measurement. **Measured in the built app** (Olive Oil v1, Record another then Add tasting, the log's width forced by CSS in 1px steps, WebKit and Chrome, fine and coarse pointer: identical in all four): the wide arrangement (row-major, three columns, 186px track, 38 x 32 stops, or 38 x 44 coarse) is three equal columns that each need 198 (the 186 track and the declared column's 12px clearance), so its frame needs **594**: clean at 594, 0.33px over at 593, no two axes overlapping down to there. The stacked arrangement (core then declared, 216px track, 44 x 44 stops) needs **216**, the track: clean at 216, 1px over at 215. While recording the frame is capped at 640, so the wide columns are 213.3 each at every width from 724 up. **The real widths** (log, then the frame inside it): 724 → 660, 640; 983 → 919, 640; 984 → 696, 640; 1365 → 1,077, 640; below 724 the log is the window and its own padding is 2 x 20 (650 → 650, 610; 640 → 640, 600); from 1366 the log is the 350 column and the frame is 350 (the stacked arrangement, 134 to spare; no overflow in the forced sweep down to a 220 frame, so none at the old 340 and 300 columns either). **The sum:** wide frame 594 + the log's own 2 x 20 below 724 = **634**, so the wide arrangement holds at every window from 634 up, and with the 8px log padding and the 2 x 32 log inset from 724 up it holds with 46px to spare at 724 (frame 640 against 594). The derived cut is therefore 634, below the ladder's 724, and the Grouping Rule folds it onto **724**, where the list-form table, the 20px margin and the stacked band already change. That moves the record pen's wide-to-stacked change, today at 760, down 36px and leaves the recipe route's width cuts as the ladder's three (1366, 984 and 724) and nothing else. What changes between 724 and 759: the battery reads in three columns with the 186 track instead of the stacked 216 track with 44 x 44 stops (38 x 44 on a coarse pointer); the touch-only caption reserve and Clear's overflowing hit area (the wide-touch block, today `min-width: 760px`) start at 724 too. **What it costs:** three literals in the app (the width-only block, the wide-touch block and `useBelow760`'s query) and the tests that pin them (`cross-cutting.test.js`, `binder.test.js`, `BatchRow.test.jsx`), plus DESIGN.md's responsive ladder paragraph, which still says "kept at 760". **For Mark:** move the cut from 760 to 724 (recommended: it is derived, it removes the route's one underived cut, and nothing between 724 and 759 overflows), or keep 760 (nothing breaks; the cut stays the one the ladder does not derive). Not drawn: the wide arrangement (724 to 1365, the full-width log) in the App skin of decision 29, which draws the stacked arrangement only. **Device-unverified:** Mark's iPad and iPhone were not used; the engines are Playwright WebKit and system Chrome. **Addendum, drawn (Sid, 2026-10-02; decision 28 is still proposed, nothing here is approved):** `724-pen-range.html`, `740-pen-range.html` and `759-pen-range.html` draw the record pen at those three window widths, each with four panels: the app today (cut at 760) on the left, the cut at 724 on the right, mouse on top, touch below. The markup is the built app's own, captured at a 740 window from two builds of the app (`pen-capture.json` keys `range_today`, `range_today_touch`, `range_cut724`, `range_cut724_touch`): the app as it stands, and a scratch copy outside `app/` with the pen's three literals moved from 760 to 724; the App skin of decision 29 is on all four panels (`SKIN` in `pen.py`, unchanged). Because a canvas artboard answers the viewer's window, `pen.py` resolves the app's `@media` blocks at each panel's window width and pointer and scopes each panel's stylesheet to the panel, so a viewer's window or pointer cannot change a board. **Measured in the built app** (WebKit and system Chrome, 724, 740 and 759, mouse and touch; the log is 660, 676 and 695 wide and the pen frame is 640 at all three, so the three boards differ only in the margin around the pen): *today* the pen is stacked, 216 track, 44 x 44 stops, and 1,469 tall (mouse) or 1,764 (touch) in WebKit, 1,460 and 1,759 in Chrome; *cut at 724* it is three columns (the Every recipe and This recipe only columns side by side), 186 track, 38 x 32 stops (38 x 44 on touch), and 1,209 tall (mouse) or 1,443 (touch) in WebKit, 1,200 and 1,440 in Chrome, which is about 260 shorter on a mouse and 320 on touch. On touch the cut also makes the caption line 28.8 tall with Clear's 44 hit area overflowing it (today 44 with Clear at 44). Neither arrangement overflows at any of the three widths: the frame's overflow, a track past its axis, an overlap between axes and the page's horizontal overflow all measure 0 in both engines and both pointers. **Conformance:** each of the 24 panels (3 widths, 2 arrangements, 2 pointers, 2 engines) against the running app's same state: 277 or 278 elements, 0 more than 0.5px off (largest difference 0.00). **Not drawn:** a window narrower than 724 (the phone forms are unchanged by the cut), a window of 760 and up (unchanged by the cut), the pen's closed tasting state, the read state. **Device-unverified:** Mark's iPad and iPhone were not used; an iPad mini in portrait is 744 wide and coarse, so it is the real device in this range. It does not change at 1366, where the Sheet narrows again. So the table form would be: stacked below 724, columns 724 to 983 (the one-column Sheet gives the table 564 to 820 and Mark found those widths good), stacked from 984 up. **What the Grouping Rule says:** a change shares a cut where the set values allow, and 984 is one. The price is the roomy stretches (1141 to 1365 and 1525 up) that get the stacked form without needing it. **Not drawn, and it would add a threshold:** a form that follows the table's own width, switching at about 488 (a container query, `@container` on the ingredient region), would leave the roomy stretches as they are and make the same pixels as C in the narrow bands. It is not a width cut and it would not move the ladder, but it is a fourth threshold that no sum derives and that no other layout change shares, so by the Grouping Rule it needs Mark's yes as a new cut, and it would be the first container query in the app (Safari 16 and later; Mark's iPad system version was not checked). A line on the "up to 1024 only" reading: the table's width at 1366 (378.7) is the same as at 984, narrower than at 1024 (405.3) and 1365 (632), so a form that stops at 1365 fixes 1024 and leaves the iPad landscape case unfixed.
**Recommendation: C, from 984 up.** It is the only form that fixes 1366 without changing a state that works: no name wraps at 1366 or 1024 in any state measured, the name column is 237 (238) at 1366 and 264 (265) at 1024 against 95 (111) and 122 (138), the reading view with no batch is the same table to within 5px, it keeps the head, and it adds no cut. The cost is height in the stacked states (about 160 to 290 px on the Sheet at 984 to 1365 with Show changes on and a batch in view, none at 1366 in that state) and a table that is columns from 724 to 983 and stacked on either side of it. **A** is the smaller step if Mark prefers to keep As made in its column: a selector change only, no layout change; at 1366 one name (Dried Skimmed Milk Powder) still wraps to 2 rows. **B** has no advantage over C except that it is the phone form exactly, and it removes the head from every state at 984 and up.
**For Mark:** (1) C, A or B; (2) whether the stacked form runs from 984 up through the widest windows (no new cut, the roomy stretches pay in height) or stops short (a new cut, or the container query); (3) the As made head: in C it is dropped, as on the phone; a head of its own would put a second line in the head row; (4) whether the stacked form should also reach 724 to 983 for one form across the iPad, at the cost of the white space Mark rejected there (decision 16). **Not drawn or not measured:** the pen and the recording state in the stacked form (the grams field, the remove link and the as-made field in the 64 track, as decisions 25 and 26 draw them at the phone, but not measured at 984 and up); the Total row beyond Mexican Chocolate, Mocha and Olive Oil; a very long name (Graza Drizzle extra virgin olive oil) in B and C; print; windows from 724 to 983 for A, B and C, which the proposal does not apply there (measured anyway: the table form keeps every name on one row, name 280 at 724 and 536 at 980; A, B and C would give 374, 434 and 422 at 724, and a table taller by 210, 488 and 463 with Show changes on). **Device-unverified:** Mark's iPad was not used; both engines are Playwright WebKit and system Chrome, which do not reproduce the device's text rendering. The earlier 03.5 boards at 984 and up (`984-batch`, `1024-batch`, `1366-batch`, `1600-batch`, `1920-batch`, `1600-no-batch`) would need to be redrawn if C is approved. **Found, then fixed:** `pen.py` could no longer regenerate: its `_cut724` asserted the app still carried the pen's old 760 literals, which decision 28 moved to 724. Fixed the same day in its own commit (the range panels are built from the app as it stands: "cut at 724" is the app's stylesheet, "today, cut at 760" moves the pen's two literals back to 760, each asserted once); no pen snapshot was regenerated, and regenerating them now would also bring in the app's other stylesheet changes since 2026-10-02 (`ingredientopts.py` still carries its own copy of the helpers).

**Option D, drawn the same day (2026-10-03, awaiting Mark's look; Sid).** Mark's words: "I want to consider another option: instead of stacking 3, stack one below, and place one to the left; we should be consistent and always put show change in same relative position and the pen version in same relative position." **Reading, confirmed against the phone and the measurements:** the three figures that compete for the amount are the plan amount, As made and the struck old figure. The old figure cannot go below the new one: the Strike Rule puts the old value before the new (decision 24 stands it above, the table draws it to the left), so it is the figure that goes *left* or *above*, and As made is the figure that goes *below* (the phone's place) or *left*. That gives two assignments, both drawn: **D1** the struck figure to the left of the plan amount on its line (as the table draws it today, in the share too) and As made below the plan amount on its right edge; **D2** the struck figure above the plan amount (decisions 24 and 25, the phone's place, in the share too) and As made to the left of it in its own track under its own head. A third, **D1b**, is D1 with one change I added because D1 wraps names: the share's struck figure stands above the current one (decision 24's stack) instead of to its left, so the struck figure is left in one column and above in the other; it is not Mark's rule applied everywhere, which is why D1 stays drawn. All three keep C's head, rules and 64 | name | share tracks (D1 and D1b widen the amount track to 108 while a struck figure is on the table), and put each figure in a named grid area, so a figure has one place in every state. The pen field is the plan amount: its " g" unit stands on the plan amount's right edge exactly where the reading view's amount ends (70 in every form), the struck parent stands immediately left of the field (D1) or above it (D2, as decision 25 draws it).
**Numbers** (WebKit, Chrome in brackets; Mexican Chocolate v3, a batch in view with constructed As made figures, Show changes on; `ingredientopts-d-measure.mjs`, `ingredientopts-d-measure.json`, 984, 1024, 1204 and 1366, coarse; the table is the same on a fine pointer):

| Form | 1366 name | 1366 wraps | 1366 table | 1024 name | 1024 wraps | 1024 table |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Today | 91 (107) | 6 names, 4 rows (Chrome 4, 3) | 808 (748) | 118 (134) | 3 names, 2 rows (Chrome 2) | 700 (658) |
| C | 237 (238) | none | 945 | 264 (265) | none | 945 |
| D1 struck left, As made below | 146 (148) | 1 name, 2 rows | 723 (722) | 172 (175) | 1 name, 2 rows (Chrome none) | 705 |
| D1b as D1, share stacked | 193 (194) | none | 721 | 220 (221) | none | 721 |
| D2 struck above, As made left | 171 (172) | 1 name, 2 rows | 746 | 198 (199) | none | 746 |

The name that wraps in D1 and D2 is Dried Skimmed Milk Powder (2 rows). At 984 the figures are the same as at 1366 (the table is 378.7 wide in both). **The Sheet's height at 984, 1024 and 1366** (WebKit, Chrome in brackets, the same state): today 1,388 (1,306), 1,246 (1,183), 1,949 (1,843); C 1,526 (1,503), 1,492 (1,470), 1,949 (1,843); D1 1,304 (1,280), 1,252 (1,230), 1,949 (1,843); D1b 1,302 (1,279), 1,268 (1,246), 1,949 (1,843); D2 1,326 (1,303), 1,292 (1,270), 1,949 (1,843). At 1366 the Sheet is the same height in every form (the Balance column sets it), so the log beside it does not move; at 984 D1b is 224px shorter than C (D1 222, D2 200) and 86px shorter than today, and at 1024 224px shorter than C (D1 240, D2 200) and 22px taller than today. **The other states** (1366, WebKit, name column / rows / table): Show changes with no batch: today 146 / 2 / 625, C 237 / 1 / 725, D1 146 / 2 / 584, D1b 193 / 1 / 679, D2 237 / 1 / 693. A batch in view and no changes: today 185 / 2 / 592, C and D1 and D1b 237 / 1 / 705 to 711, D2 171 / 2 / 585 (D2's As made track costs more than today's As made column, 56 + the gap against 51). The pen with three amounts changed: today 138 / 2 / 1,135, C 237 / 1 / 909, D1 146 / 2 / 921, D1b 193 / 1 / 905, D2 237 / 1 / 883. The recording pen (Olive Oil v1): today 172 / 1 / 1,020, C 237 / 1 / 1,297, D1 and D1b 237 / 1 / 1,291, D2 171 / 1 / 1,031 (D2 keeps the As made field on the plan amount's line, so a recording row is not taller than today's; C, D1 and D1b put the 44px field on a second line and add 270px). Page overflow 0 in every cell.
**Do the figures hold their places?** (WebKit, 1366, relative to the plan amount; the same at 1024.) *D1:* the struck figure sits on the plan amount's line immediately to its left in the reading view, with As made, with a batch and in the pen (dy 0 in all; in the pen it is immediately left of the field); As made sits under the plan amount on its right edge (dx 0) in every state that has it, 17.2px below the text and 19.8px below it for the recording field; the plan amount's right edge is 70 with no struck figure on the table and 114 with one, in the reading view and in the pen alike (114 in both), the name column starting at 80 and 124. *D2:* the struck figure sits above the plan amount in every state (dy -18.2 in the reading view, -18.1 with a batch; in the pen above the field, as decision 25 draws it); As made sits in its own track 74px left of the plan amount's right edge and level with it (dy -2.7, -0.1 for the recording field) in every state that has it; the plan amount's right edge is 70 without the As made track and 136 with it. *C* for comparison: the struck figure above, As made below, the name column at 80 in every state. **What moves, and this is the cost of the left placement:** in D1 the name column moves 40px to the right when the first struck figure appears (80 to 124): when Show changes is turned on, and in the pen the moment an amount is changed, because the amount track grows. In D2 the name column moves 66px when the As made track is present: from 80 in the pen (which carries no As made column) to 146 with a batch in view. In C nothing moves horizontally in any state. A drawing that kept the left slot always reserved (108px in every state) would not move but would take the name column to 237 - 44 = 193 in every state; that is D1b's width, with nothing in the slot when nothing is struck.
**Ladder and cuts.** D adds no cut: it takes C's place, from 984 up, with the same named tracks. D1 and D1b use `:has()` on the table (the amount track widens when a struck figure is on the table; Safari 15.4 and later), and D1, D1b and D2 use `:has()` for the As made track; the app would carry a modifier class on the table instead, a build detail.
**What D means for the recommendation.** On names, C is still the only form that wraps nothing at both widths and moves nothing horizontally, and D2 is the only one that keeps recording rows as short as today's. If Mark prefers the two-axis rule, **D1b** is the form of the pair that works: no name wraps at 1366 or 1024, its table is 224px shorter than C's, and the price is the 40px shift when a struck figure appears and a struck figure that stands left in the amount and above in the share. D1 as Mark worded it (left in both) wraps one name at 1366 and 984 (name 146) because the share's struck figure takes about 47px more than D1b's; D2 wraps one name at 1366 (name 171) because the As made track sits in the name's budget. **For Mark (added):** (5) C, D1b, D1 or D2; (6) in D1/D1b, whether the name column may shift 40px when Show changes turns on and when the first amount is edited in the pen (as the table's own amount column does today) or the left slot should be reserved (the name column 193 in every state); (7) for D2, whether the As made column moving to the left of the plan amount, under its own head, is the placement Mark meant by "to the left"; (8) a mixed share (D1b) or the same placement in both (D1). **Not drawn or not measured (D):** the pen at 984 to 1365 other than the measured widths, a very long name in D1 to D2, print, the iPad itself (WebKit and Chrome only).

**D3 chosen by Mark (2026-10-03): "D3 is my favorite. iPhone follows the design too. Plan / as made / struck. I want keep things static when Show changes toggles. Yes, one name in 2 rows is acceptable."** Decided: the Sheet table uses D3 from 984 up (struck old figure BELOW the plan amount, As made left of it under its own head, plan amount static when Show changes toggles); the phone follows, so decisions 24 and 25 are amended: below 724 the cell order is plan amount, then As made, then the struck figure (so As made does not move when Show changes toggles), in the amount, the share, the Total row and the pen; one name at 2 rows at 984 and 1366 is accepted. Still to do when written: Sid redraws the phone boards and amends decisions 24 and 25 and the briefs (route-recipe-version.md section 3), then the app change goes through GSD. **Done 2026-10-03 (Sid), see the paragraphs at the end of this decision:** the phone boards redrawn (drawn, awaiting Mark's look), decisions 24 and 25 amended, the briefs and DESIGN.md amended, the app change brief written. **Original note, Sid (D3 drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing approved when written):** Mark picked D2 as the direction and asked for one change (his words): "I think that D2 is the right direction, but if we place the struck amount under the plan amount, then there is less movement when Show changes is toggled; plan amount is static, and the struck amount appears/disappears underneath." **D3 = D2 with the struck old figure below the current one** in the amount, the share, the Total and the pen's parent value (under the field); As made stays left of the plan amount under its own head, now level with the plan amount without an offset. The plan amount keeps the first line of its cell. In CSS the cell is a flex column in reverse order, so the DOM stays struck-first (a reader still hears "was, now"; focus order is untouched since only the pen's field is focusable). Drawn on both boards as a column beside D2, in all four rows. **What D3 contradicts, exactly:** (1) Decision 24, approved by Mark 2026-10-02 (1b): "the struck old figure above the new one, right-aligned in the same tracks, in both the amount and the % of batch", and its Total row (struck total stacked above the current one, no unit). (2) Decision 25, approved the same day: the pen's struck parent stacks above the 52px grams field. (3) The brief's positional clause, `route-recipe-version.md` § 3 "Strike and beside": "the parent's value struck in ink immediately before the field or the current value", and the app's own comment on `.prose-struck-beneath` (app.css), which names the paragraph as "the one place the strike sits beneath rather than beside". **What D3 does not contradict:** the Strike Rule itself (`route-recipe-version.md` § 7; it is not yet recorded in DESIGN.md, which only names the strike in Print Ink): "a strike means not in force, and what sits beside it says why ... beside a superseded value, its replacement". That is a rule about meaning and adjacency: the strike has one meaning and its replacement is next to it. It says nothing of order or direction, so the Rule holds under D3 (the replacement is still next to the strike, directly above it); the position clauses in (3) are what D3 changes, and I should have said "before" where I wrote "the Strike Rule puts the old value before the new" in the D paragraph above: that wording is the brief's § 3 and the app comment's, not the Rule's. Decision 24 already moved the old figure from beside to above (still before, in reading order, top to bottom); D3 puts it after. **Wide only, or the phone too?** Mark's principle (2026-10-03, asking for D): "we should be consistent and always put show change in same relative position and the pen version in same relative position". D3 at the wide widths only would put the struck figure above on the phone (decisions 24 and 25) and below from 724 up: two relative positions, which breaks that principle. **Recommendation: if Mark takes D3, the phone follows**, because the phone's cost is one declaration (the figure's order in its cell: the phone's list form stacks it as a block above, the change is `flex-direction: column-reverse` on the same three cells; no width moves, the 223px name track at 393 does not change) and because the plan amount is the phone's anchor too. One thing the phone must settle first: its third figure. On the phone As made stands under the amount (decision 15), so under D3 a changed row with a batch reads plan, struck, As made (As made drops a line when Show changes goes on) or plan, As made, struck (As made static, the struck line appears last); neither is drawn, and Mark's word on D3 does not say which. **Boards D3 would force to be redrawn if the phone follows (not touched here):** `393-show-changes.html`, `723-show-changes.html`, `393-show-changes-cases.html` (all five panels), `393-pen-changes.html`, `723-pen-changes.html`, and the "Changes shown" panel of `393-show-changes-head.html` and `723-show-changes-head.html`; `011-options-show-changes/list-form-options.html` is the options sheet behind decision 24 (1b marked recommended) and would stay as history. Decisions 24 and 25 would need an amendment recording the reversal, and the app's phone block (app.css, the three `.struck-value { display: block }` rules in the phone forms block) would be replaced by the reverse-order rule and apply at every width.
**D3 measured** (WebKit, Chrome in brackets; Mexican Chocolate v3, a batch in view with constructed As made figures, Show changes on): name column, wraps and heights are D2's except the table: 984 and 1366: name 171 (172), 1 name wraps to 2 rows (Dried Skimmed Milk Powder), table 729 (726) against D2's 746 and C's 945; 1024: name 198 (199), no wrap, table 726 (724) against 746 and 945. The Sheet at 984: 1,310 (1,284) against D2's 1,326 (1,303) and C's 1,526 (1,503); at 1024: 1,272 (1,248) against 1,292 (1,270) and 1,492 (1,470); at 1366: 1,949 (1,843) in all three (the Balance column sets it). Show changes only (no batch): D3 and D2 are equal, name 237 (238), no wrap, table 693; the same for the pen with amounts changed (883) and the recording pen (1,031), since only the struck line moves. D3 is shorter than D2 only where As made and a struck figure share a row (D2 drops As made by one line to stay level with the plan amount). **Movement when Show changes is toggled** (reading to Show changes with no batch; a batch in view to the same with Show changes; the pen untouched to the pen with the first amount edited; 1366, WebKit): the plan amount's left and right edges, the name column, As made and the share's right edge move 0px horizontally in C, D2 and D3 alike. Vertically: in C and D2 the first row's plan amount drops 18px when Show changes goes on (the struck line is inserted above it); in D3 it moves 0px with no batch and about 1.9px with a batch in view or in the pen (the flex line box against the cell's plain line), so the plan amount is static, as Mark asked. In the pen the unit after the field measured 0.9px left in D3 (sub-pixel); the field and the name column move 0. **Vertical shift of the rows below:** each changed row grows from 32.9px to 49.0px, 16.1px per row in D2 and D3 (C 33 to 51, 18px), and the 12 changed rows and the Total add 217px to the table (C 234); in D3 that growth is all under each plan amount, so no figure above the struck line moves. **Where D3 reads badly:** (a) the new value stands first and the old one second, so "563 g, was 500 g" reads as the result and then the history, the reverse of decision 24's order; Mark may find it the better order, since the number the maker acts on comes first. (b) The Total: the struck total sits under the bold total with no unit, "911.7 g" over "772.1". (c) A removed row (constructed from the seed, checked in the app with the form injected): the lone struck amount stands on the plan amount's line and the row is one line (33px) between changed rows of 49px, so struck-at-the-top means removed and struck-below means changed; the struck amount occupies the place a plan amount has, which a quick reader may take for a plan amount. (d) In the pen the old value sits under the whole field box (44px tall on a coarse pointer), so it is below the field's full height rather than next to the number being typed. (e) The struck line sits 6px above the row's rule, so the strike and the rule read close together in the share. (f) D3 does not change the widths: the name column is D2's, so Dried Skimmed Milk Powder still wraps to 2 rows at 984 and 1366 (171). (g) Visual order differs from DOM order (the cell is a reverse flex column): fine for the one focusable control, noted for any future focusable control in these cells. **For Mark:** (9) D3 at the wide widths only (breaks the same-place principle across widths) or the phone too (recommended, with the redraws above); (10) on the phone, plan / struck / As made or plan / As made / struck; (11) the 171px name column of D2 and D3 at 984 and 1366 (one name at 2 rows) is the cost of the As made track left of the plan amount; D1b and C keep names on one row.

**Phone redrawn for D3 (2026-10-03, Sid; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing approved).** Mark chose D3 and answered (2026-10-03): "D3 is my favorite. iPhone follows the design too. Plan / as made / struck. I want keep things static when Show changes toggles. Yes, one name in 2 rows is acceptable." Redrawn from the generators (`showchanges.py`, `showchangeshead.py`, section P3 of `ingredient-options.css`): `393-show-changes`, `723-show-changes`, `393-show-changes-cases` (all five panels), `393-pen-changes`, `723-pen-changes`, and the Changes shown panel of `393-show-changes-head` and `723-show-changes-head`. **Checked and not changed:** `393-phone-log` and `723-phone-log` (their only Show changes is the band's control, no struck figure), `011-options-show-changes/list-form-options.html` (the 2026-10-02 options sheet, history). **Still showing the old position, not redrawn here:** the wide boards that draw the ingredient table in the column form (`984-batch`, `1024-batch`, `1366-batch`, `1600-batch`, `1600-no-batch`, `1600-long-history`, `1920-batch`) and `1600-pen` (which draws the parent's struck value before the field); from 984 up the table drawing is the D3 column of `1366-ingredient-options` and `1024-ingredient-options`, and those boards need a redraw when Mark asks or when the app change lands. **One cell is more than a reversed order.** Plan / As made / struck needs As made between two figures that live in one cell (the struck figure is inside the plan amount's cell, As made is the next cell), so the phone is not the one declaration I guessed when I recommended the phone follow: the amount's cell and its span give up their boxes (`display: contents`) so the struck figure can take the row grid's third line; the share's cell is a reversed flex column, and spans the three lines when the amount has a struck figure, so the two struck figures stand on one line; each lower figure carries its own hair above it instead of the grid's row gap, so an empty line adds nothing. About eight declarations (P3 in `ingredient-options.css`), DOM unchanged. **Measured in the built app below 724 with P3 injected in-page** (320, 375, 393 and 428 coarse, 723 fine and coarse; WebKit and system Chrome; `ingredientopts-phone-measure.mjs`, results `ingredientopts-phone-measure.json`; Mexican Chocolate v4 against v3, v3 with constructed As made figures, the pen on v4 with three amounts changed, a constructed removed row): when Show changes is toggled, with or without a batch, the plan amount moves 0px in both axes at every width and in both engines (today, struck above, it drops 18px), As made 0px (today 18px), and in the pen the grams field 0px when an amount is edited (today 18px); the name track is unchanged by the toggle (150.5 at 320, 205.5 at 375, 223.5 at 393, 258.5 at 428, 553.5 at 723 in WebKit; Chrome 1.2px more) and unchanged from today; page overflow 0 and cell collisions 0 in every state. **Growth:** a changed row goes from 36.9px to 55.0px (+18.1; at 320, where names wrap, 54.9 to 55); with a batch 57 to 77 (+20; today 57 to 75). The Total row 36 to 55 (with a batch 58 to 78; today 38 to 56, and 77). The table of Mexican Chocolate v4 at 393: 507 to 749 (+242; today 516 to 750); with a batch (v3, constructed As made figures) 735 to 992 (+257; today 737 to 971). The pen at 393: 899 untouched, 938 with the changes (today 915 and 951). **The share column with As made between** reads clearly: at 393 with a batch the amount column reads 563 g, 503 g in the hand, then the struck 500 g, and the share reads 61.8% and, on the same last line as the struck amount (y 83.6 for both), the struck 64.8%; a share that moved alone keeps its struck figure directly under it. The price is one blank line (about 17px, the As made line) between a share and its struck figure in the batch rows whose amount changed; I did not draw the other option, the share's struck figure on the second line beside As made and off the amount's struck line. Where it reads badly: the removed row's lone struck amount stands on the first line, where a plan amount stands (a quick reader may take it for a plan amount); with a batch the struck figure is two lines below the number it replaces. **Board against app:** the redrawn show-changes, pen and head boards against the built app with P3 injected, 292 laid-out cells, 2 engines, 0 more than 0.5px off (`ingredientopts-phone-conform.mjs`; the head boards' Coconut capture still draws a lone Unallocated step head that the app has hidden since quick task 261002-wdn, so those two compare without it). **Open, not decided: 724 to 983.** Mark's D3 is "from 984 up" and the phone; the column table at 724 to 983 (the one-column Sheet, where the table has room) stays as built, with the struck figure beside the plan amount (before it). That gives three positions across the ladder: struck below below 724, beside from 724 to 983, below from 984 up, which is against Mark's "same relative position" principle. If Mark wants one position, D3 reaches 724 to 983 as well (name column 374 at 724 with a batch and Show changes on, more above it; nothing wraps).

**App change brief (for a GSD plan; nothing here edits `app/`).** Source of truth: the 1366 and 1024 `ingredient-options` boards (column D3) from 984 up, and the redrawn phone boards below 724; the CSS to read is `.planning/canvas-generators/ingredient-options.css`, sections D, D2, D3 (984 and up) and P3 (below 724); every board number above was measured on the built app with those rules injected, so the plan can use them as acceptance numbers.
1. **984 and up, the D3 grid** (`app/src/styles/app.css`, `IngredientTable.jsx`): the table keeps its `<table>` and its head and rules; each `tbody` and `tfoot` row is a grid with named areas, `plan name share` and, when the version has an As made layer, `asm plan name share`; tracks `--sheet-field-w-figure` (56) | `--sheet-plan-grams-w` (64) | `minmax(0, 1fr)` | `max-content`, `column-gap: var(--sheet-narrow-name-gap)`, row padding `var(--gap-xs) var(--sheet-table-cell-pad-x)`, the table's own rule weight. As made sits left of the plan amount in its own track, level with it, under its own head ("As made" th over the track, "Ingredient" th over the plan amount and the name, "% of batch" right). The struck old figure stands under the current one in the amount, the share and the Total (flex column-reverse on the cells that hold a struck figure; DOM order unchanged, struck first). The table gets two modifier classes from `IngredientTable.jsx` instead of the boards' `:has()` (As made layer present; a struck figure present), or uses `:has()` if Mark's iPad is on Safari 15.4 or later (not checked). The amount's right padding (`--sheet-plan-grams-gap`) goes in this form.
2. **Below 724, the phone's rule** (`app.css` phone-forms block): replace the three `.struck-value { display: block; margin-right: 0 }` rules (`.ingredient-table__plan-grams > .struck-value`, `td.ingredient-table__col-numeric > .struck-value`, `td.ingredient-table__col-grams > .struck-value`) with P3: row gap 0, each lower figure carrying `margin-top: var(--gap-hair)`; the name cell spans three rows; the amount's cell and its plan-grams span (when it holds a struck figure) `display: contents`; the struck figure on grid row 3 (row 1 for a removed row's lone struck amount); the share's cell a reversed flex column, spanning the three rows when the amount has a struck figure; the row's `text-align: right` (an anonymous box takes its alignment from the row) and the name cell `text-align: left`.
3. **The share, the Total row and the pen:** the share's struck figure on the amount's last line (above); the Total's struck bare number under the bold total, no unit, last after its As made total; the pen's struck parent (a direct child of the amount cell, before the field) under the 52px grams field, last line, the field staying on the plan amount's line (44px tall on a coarse pointer, so the old value sits under the whole field box); a removed row in the pen keeps its field first and the struck parent under it; the remove and restore links keep decision 26's 14px gap.
4. **724 to 983:** unchanged unless Mark extends D3 there (open, above).
5. **Print:** leave print alone; Phase 04 owns it. The `@media print` block in app.css does not touch the ingredient table; the new screen rules must not change what the printed Sheet draws, so scope them to screen or confirm Phase 04's print rules override them.

7. **The app.css comment** on `.prose-struck-beneath` ("the one place the strike sits beneath rather than beside") is app territory and is now wrong: below is how every figure stands, the paragraph remains the one place the strike is a separate paragraph. Listed here, not edited. The old comment on `.struck-value` ("immediately before the value that replaced it") likewise.

32. **Mark chose candidate g, the fly-out nav, on 2026-10-03 (his answer to the first question: "G"). Mark's answers on 2026-10-03: (1) "Wordmark is a link. Button is pinned": the Sprinkles wordmark becomes a link to Home and the fly-out's menu button is pinned (sticky, 44px) so it stays on screen; (2) "Yes": As made is the first column on tablet and paper, stacked under the plan amount on the phone; (3) "Yes": the writing column is 56px on screen and at least 72px on paper; (4) "Don't reserve": with no batch the As made column is absent on screen and appears with the batch (the plan amount and names shift right by 66px when it does); (5) "Need to discuss this", then "Option A": D3 reaches 724-983 too (the struck figure under the plan amount, As made first), so the struck figure is under the plan amount at every width from 724 up and in the stacked phone order below; **Phone boards approved by Mark (2026-10-03, "Phone boards approved"):** the seven phone boards redrawn for D3 (393-show-changes, 723-show-changes, 393-show-changes-cases, 393-pen-changes, 723-pen-changes, and the Changes shown panels of 393-show-changes-head and 723-show-changes-head), with the order plan / As made / struck, including the blank line between a share and its struck figure when a batch is in view. (6) "Goto batch extends above": the Go to batch row extends above 724, wherever the batch log sits below the Sheet. (7) "Balance folds open": Balance's folds are open wherever the Balance column is beside the ingredients (Sid's working assumption confirmed); (8) "Rule A for 724-983" (2026-10-03): the sticky header and the fly-out are the shell from 724 up and the bottom tab row stays only below 724; the 744, 834 and 983 boards (drawn with Rule B) must be redrawn to Rule A; (9) the wide windows keep the rail ("I thought that at 1600, 1920 that we would have the rail and that the fly-out was only necessary at certain widths"): the rail returns at 1590 and above, the header and fly-out apply from 724 to 1589.** What g means, from Sid's measurements: no rail in the Sheet's sums, so the Sheet minimum is 920 and the table 528 at 984 and 1366 with the log beside at 1366; the cuts stay 984 and 1366 (L's new 1590 cut is not needed); every place is two taps; decision 27's pinned options go while the menu is closed unless the menu button is pinned; decision 16's sums and the sticky nav boards are reopened. **Original note, Sid (drawn 2026-10-03, awaiting Mark's look; nothing approved when written): The recipe route's four columns on the iPad Pro: which one gives way first.** Mark's challenge (2026-10-03): "My motivation for this whole arc is this: on the iPad Pro landscape, we have 4 columns: nav, ingredients, balance, batch; that forces ingredients to be narrow. Rather than pushing balance below we are choosing a narrow ingredients column. At other ladder checkpoints, we don't have 4 columns in a constrained line width and we should design accordingly. Perhaps I should challenge Sid on this: at what horizontal size should we disallow all 4 columns, and what is priority order of which columns drop first." And his addition: "Collapsing the side nav gives more space back to the ingredients table, and the side nav is a click-and-go whereas the recipe sections are for specific jobs." Everything below was measured on the built app (dist of 2026-10-03, Playwright WebKit and system Chrome, coarse pointer, throwaway 127.0.0.1 servers, no Vite, nothing on :4173 or :8011; `ladder-measure.mjs`, `ladder-log-nav.mjs`, results `ladder-measure.json`, `ladder-log-nav.json`) with candidate rules moved in-page. Mexican Chocolate v3, a batch in view, Show changes on, constructed As made figures (the seed has none); Chrome is within 16px on name columns, same wraps unless stated. Device-unverified: Mark's iPad was not used.
**1. The ladder as built** (boards on the canvas as version 287) (DESIGN.md, decisions 16, 27, 28, 31). Set values: side nav 224 (plus its hairline) from 984, Sheet two-column minimum 696, log beside 350, gutters 32, content maximum 1482 (Sheet 1100) centred from 1770; the Sheet's columns split 2fr and 1fr after its 48px margins and 32 gap, so table = (Sheet - 128) x 2/3 and Balance = (Sheet - 128) / 3. Cuts: 1366 = 224 + 3 x 32 + 696 + 350 (log beside), 984 = 224 + 2 x 32 + 696 (rail and two-column Sheet, together), 724 (phone forms). Precedence (decision 16): the log moves below first, then the rail and the second column together. Four columns exist only from 1366; from 984 to 1365 it is three (rail, ingredients, Balance, the log below). Decision 27 pins the rail's options (6px under the window's top); decision 28 the record pen's cut (724, wide arrangement 594, stacked 216); decision 31 the table's D3 form from 984 up and the phone's P3.

**2. Minimum widths, from the evidence.** *Ingredients table:* no seeded name wraps in the stacked figure forms from 394 (A and D3 forms, Mexican Chocolate v3 with a batch and Show changes) and in today's column form from 488 (WebKit; 472 Chrome). I take **528** (name 240 in the column form, 334 stacked): the width at which today's table, as built, draws every seeded name on one row with a batch and Show changes on. *Balance:* 190 is decision 18's floor; at 189 the open Balance and its rules are 1,131px tall, at 214 1,084, at 264 979, at 289 949, at 324 934 (Mexican Chocolate v3): the column keeps getting shorter to about 260, so **264** (= a 920 Sheet). *Batch log:* nothing clips or overflows down to 240 (reading and the record pen with Add tasting open; `ladder-log-nav.json`), but the pen grows taller as it narrows (2,483px at 350, 2,533 at 320, 2,583 at 300, 2,736 at 260), so **350** stays (decision 16), 300 would be the floor with +100px of pen. *Side nav:* the widest place, 'Recipe book', is icon 20 + gap 6 + label 79.8 + the place's own 40 of padding = 146, and the rail adds 40, so 186 is the narrowest rail in the row form (saving 38), 84 with the icon over the label (the tab row's own place style turned vertical, drawn as e; saving 140); the bottom tab row takes no width and 56px of height. **Four columns stop being allowed below 1590** = 224 + 3 x 32 + 350 + 920 (the rail's 224 added to the 1366 sum with a 920 Sheet). By the table at 1366 itself: no, 1366 should not keep four columns (table 379, name column 91 in today's form with a batch and Show changes, 171 in D3).
**3. The drop order, and a position.** Mark's lean is that the nav gives way first, because it is used to go somewhere while the recipe's columns are each a job. **I agree**, and the measurements say how much it buys: the rail is the only column that is not doing the maker's work on the page, it is 16% of a 1366 window against the tab row's 5.5% of a 1024 height (56 of 1024), and width is what the Sheet is short of while height is what it scrolls. Then, as the window narrows: **(1) the rail becomes the tab row, (2) the log goes below the Sheet, (3) Balance goes below the ingredients, (4) the phone forms.** The reason for the order between the last three is the maker's job: formulating (the ingredients with Balance beside them, 'a glance, not a scroll' with the focus trace; app.css, the Balance column's own comment) is the job done most; checking Balance goes with it; transcribing a batch comes from the paper sheet (decisions 14 and 30) and is reached by the jump on the phone, so the log can wait below the Sheet before Balance does. The honest qualification: while recording a batch the as-made fields are in the table and the readings are in the log, so the two want to be on screen together, and the log below is 1,300px (Mexican Chocolate v3) to 2,290px (Olive Oil v1) below where it stands beside; that is the price of dropping it second, and it is today's price at 984 to 1365. **Candidates at 1366, each the app's own rule moved** (boards `1366-ladder` and `1194-ladder`, today's column table in every panel; WebKit; page heights with the folds open, closed in brackets):

| | Rail | Sheet | Table | Balance | Log | Name column (batch + changes) | Page, Mex (Olive) | Balance from the Ingredients heading | Log heading y |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| a today | 224 | 696 | 378.7 | 189 beside | 350 beside | 91, 6 names wrap, 4 rows | 2,608 (3,084) [2,047] | 0 | 611 (422) |
| b Balance below | 224 | 696 | 600 | below | 350 beside | 312, none | 2,812 (3,845) [1,796] | 566 (779) | 611 (422) |
| c log below | 224 | 1,077 | 632.7 | 316 beside | below | 345, none | 2,219 (3,174) [1,683] | 0 | 1,911 (2,710) |
| d tab row | 0 (tab row) | 920 | 528 | 264 beside | 350 beside | 240, none | 2,377 (2,877) [1,736] | 0 | 611 (422) |
| e narrow rail | 84 | 836 | 472 | 236 beside | 350 beside | 184, 1 name, 2 rows | 2,441 (2,859) [1,783] | 0 | 611 (422) |

What each costs: **b** gives the table +221 and takes Balance 566px (Olive Oil 779) below the ingredients heading, so the focus trace and the rules are a scroll away from the rows they mark, and the Sheet is 204px taller open (2,812 against 2,608) though 251px shorter closed; **c** gives +254 and Balance +127, and the log leaves the screen the maker is on; **d** gives +149 (table 528, Balance 264) and costs the rail's words, the tab row's 56px of height and, as built, Search, Import and Export (the tab-row block folds them into More; d keeps them in the header) and Ingredients and Kitchen (behind More: two taps instead of one); **e** gives +93 (472) and keeps a rail, but leaves the column table one name at two rows (472 is under 488) unless the figures stack. **What a collapsed nav should look like:** the bottom tab row that already exists (icon over word, 5 slots), with three changes: it lists all six places at tablet widths instead of four and More (6 slots across 724 or more is 120px each), the header keeps Search, Import and Export one tap away (they fold into More today only because the header is crowded on a phone, so the fold moves to the 724 phone cut and groups with it), and it stays fixed at the bottom. Why not the others: an icon-only rail drops the words (plain words on screen) and has no hover on a touch screen; a top bar either costs 44 to 56px of height at the top for good or scrolls away, which stops it being click-and-go, and the places in the header row (wordmark 150 + six places about 600 + tools about 280) do not fit below about 1030; a toggle that overlays the page makes every place two taps, which is not click-and-go. **Decision 27** (the pinned rail options) is met by a fixed bar by construction and applies only where the rail stays (1590 and up); the narrow rail e would keep it. **The 984 cut:** the rail no longer arrives there; see the ladder below.

Two rule changes and one set value: (i) the rail yields first, so it is drawn only where it fits beside the three job columns; (ii) the Sheet's two-column minimum goes from 696 to **920**; the sums keep their windows: 984 = 2 x 32 + 920 (the Sheet's second column, now without the rail's 224), 1366 = 3 x 32 + 920 + 350 (the log beside), and the rail returns at **1590 = 224 + 1366**. The cuts are 1590, 1366, 984 and 724: three of today's windows stay, the 724 phone cut is untouched, and one is new. Below 724 nothing changes. **What L reopens, and what would need redrawing.** *Decision 16:* the Sheet's two-column minimum (696), the precedence ('the rail and the second column go together' becomes 'the rail yields first, alone') and the sums of 984 and 1366; the content maximum (1482) already applies to the log-beside row only; with the log below the Sheet is not capped in the app as built. *Decision 27:* the sticky rail stays but only from 1590; `1366-sticky-nav` and `984-sticky-nav` draw a rail where L has none, so they are superseded and a 1600 board takes their place. *Decision 28:* untouched (the pen's cut is 724 and the log keeps 350 beside). *Decision 18:* the folds open from 1366; under L Balance is beside from 984, so its folds should open wherever the column is beside (from 984), not from 1366; that is a question. *Decision 31 and the table drawing:* the 984-up grid is not needed (above). *DESIGN.md:* the ladder table and the cut sums, and shell.css's D-16 (the tab row's block folds Search, Import and Export into More at 983). *Boards to redraw:* `984-batch`, `1024-batch`, `1366-batch`, `984-sticky-nav`, `1366-sticky-nav` (no rail), `1600-batch` family unchanged, and the two `ingredient-options` boards (their table widths become 528 and 555). *App:* `shell.css` (the rail's cut, the tab row to six places, the header tools), `app.css` (the Sheet's cut and cap), `notebook.css` (the fold and band rhythm cuts follow), `useBelowDesktop`, and the tests that pin 984 and 1366 as media literals.
**For Mark:** (1) L, or keep the rail with the narrow rail e (table 472 at 1366, one name at two rows in the column form), or the ladder as it is; (2) the tab row listing all six places at tablet widths and the header's Search, Import and Export staying until 724; (3) the Sheet's two-column minimum at 920 (table 528) or lower (780 gives 435, which needs the stacked figure form, and moves the cuts to 844, 1226 and 1450); (4) with the log below up to 1365, whether the 'Go to batch' jump should extend above 724 (today it stops at 724); (5) D3's As made left of the plan amount, or the column table with the struck figure under (L makes either work); (6) Balance's folds open wherever the column is beside. **Not drawn or not measured:** the 1024 and 1440 windows (numbers only, measured), a top bar and an overlay (argued, not drawn), the recording pen and the pen under L, print, the iPad itself.
