The cases the rule must cover are the first five (count, state, date, date then actions); the sixth to ninth are actions whose job is to sit at an edge. **Adjacent, not part of this rule:** the band's two columns (the title block at the left, the Version block starting at about 700px at 1600, empty between) is a layout gap, not a label and its info; Sid flags it for Mark and does not draw it here.
**What the sketch says today:** decision 19 ("any count or date at the row's end"), decision 20 (Tasting's date at the end so its Show sits straight after the label), decision 22 (counts carry only the count), decision 30 (the Go to batch row takes History's row grammar and now extends from 724 to 1365). They were drawn at 393 and 723, where the far end is 128 to 244px away; the wide boards of decision 33 inherited the same CSS and the gap grew to 514 to 1,321.
**Four options, one rule moved per row (new boards, the built app's own markup, cropped to the rows; the gap is measured in each caption).** `info-labels-bands.html`: the band at 744, 1024, 1366, 1600 and 1920 (History, Go to batch). `info-labels-log.html`: the log at 744, 1024 and the 350 column from 1366 (batch head, Tasting). Row 1 is the boards as drawn; rows 2 to 4 are the options.
- **A, start-aligned.** From 724, every head and fold row reads from the left: label, control word, a dot, then the count, state or date; the batch head runs the date, then the actions 32px later. Gap 32 at every width (History 514 to 1,321 becomes 32; Go to batch 173 and 295 become 32; Tasting 439 and 719 become 32; the batch head 289 and 569 become 32). The whole row is still the tap target (nothing changes in the button). The dot keeps "Hide" from reading as a verb on the count ("Hide 4 versions"); the accessible name is unchanged ("History, Hide, 4 versions").
- **B, capped row.** The far end stays the far end but no head or fold row is wider than the log column (350). History 184 to 189, Go to batch 173 and 241, Tasting 125 to 130, the batch head wraps (actions under the date, as in the 350 column today). The row, and so its tap target, is 350 wide instead of the band's width.
- **C, leader.** The far end stays and a dotted leader (`--rule-baseline`, `--app-text-secondary`) runs from the control word to the count, state or date. The gaps are the same as today (514 to 1,321) but connected by a line that is up to 1,300px long; the batch head is as drawn.
- **D, the info under the label (not drawn):** each row grows from 44 to about 62px (the info a second line at 724 and up), four rows in the band and the log; it fixes the gap and costs height on a page already 2.4 to 4.5 screens. **E, the count before the control (not drawn):** "History · 4 versions  Hide"; it breaks decision 20's rule that Show sits straight after the label.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): A, by role, from 724 up.** Count, state and date follow the control word after a dot; the batch head's actions follow the date. The exceptions keep the far end because they have their own grammar and a reason to sit at an edge: table figures (hairlines), the Balance figure (a key number in a column of at most 320), the header's tools and the pen's Cancel and Save (action blocks at an edge), and every action in a 350 column (it wraps). Reasons: one rule covers all five cases with one number (32), no row width is invented (B needs a cap that a 350 log column already is but a band is not), no decoration is added (C), no height is lost (D), and decision 20's Show-after-label order stands. **Where it applies:** from 724, the cut the ladder already has; below 724 the far end is 128 to 244px away and the approved phone boards stand unchanged (the phone's rows are one thumb wide). **With the rail (1590 and up)** and **with the header and fly-out (724 to 1589):** no interplay; the rows are inside the main area and A is measured at 1600 and 1920 (with the rail) and at 744, 1024 and 1366 (with the header). The header's tools stay at the window's edge by convention. **Touch targets:** the fold row is still one full-width, 44px-tall button (decision 18, G-03.5-4); in A the blank end of the row is tappable as it is now; in B the target shrinks to 350, which is the one real cost of B.
**What it touches (approved decisions):** 19 (counts at the row's end), 20 (Tasting's date at the end), 22 (counts only: unchanged, the text stays the same), 30 (the Go to batch row's status at the end) and the batch head's space-between from 03.5-17. **Redrawn if adopted:** every wide board that carries a History, Go to batch, Tasting or batch head row: `744-batch`, `834-batch`, `983-batch`, `984-batch`, `1024-batch`, `1366-batch`, `1600-batch`, `1600-pen`, `1600-no-batch`, `1600-long-history`, `1920-batch`, `724-1365-go-to-batch`, the four sticky-nav boards (the page behind the bar), plus the measures table's captions (heights do not change). The 393 and 723 boards are untouched. **App files (not edited):** `app/src/styles/notebook.css` (a `(min-width: 724px)` block: `.notebook .fold-row`, `.notebook-jump`, `.notebook-log .batch-row__head`; the dot as a `::before` on `.fold-row__count` and `.notebook-jump__status`, or as markup), `app/src/ui/FoldRow.jsx` and `GoToBatch.jsx` only if the dot is markup, and the tests that pin the rows (`notebook.test.js`, `cross-cutting.test.js`). It is brief task 9 in decision 33's split, independent of the others.
**Questions for Mark:** (1) A, B, C or leave as drawn? (2) With A, the dot between the control word and the count, or none? (3) In the batch head, should Correct and Record another follow the date (A), or stay at the end where, in the 350 column, they already wrap under it? (4) Apply from 724 (recommended) or at every width, the phone included? (5) The band's title-to-Version gap at 1366 and up: a separate decision for Sid to draw? **Mark's answers (2026-10-04):** (1) A, start-aligned. (2) Use dots to separate. (3) Correct and Record another follow the date. (4) Every width, not only from 724. (5) Draw a separate decision for the band's title-to-Version gap at 1366 and up. **Mark's later answers (2026-10-04, after quick 261004-igr built from 724 up):** the dot-separated labels apply on the phone too, and Sid draws 393 and 723 first; the batch head keeps its height at 1366 and up, and Sid redraws row A with a 16px gap between lines.

35. **The Sheet title in the Next version pen is one field (Sid, 2026-10-04; Mark: "I noticed in the Next version on iPhone that in the Sheet, the Sheet Title is shown as text then again as a text edit field directly beneath. The Sheet description is just a single editable field. Seems like title should be a single editable field also."; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is approved).**
**What the app draws today (measured in the built app, WebKit, Olive Oil v1 and Mexican Chocolate v4 with Next version open, at 393, 744, 1024, 1366 and 1600; `title-probe.mjs`, `title-probe.json`).** `app/src/ui/Headnote.jsx`: line 23 renders `<h1>{version.sheetTitle}</h1>` in every mode; in the developing mode a labelled field follows it (line 34), `<input type="text" class="ink-field" aria-label="Sheet title">` bound to the draft, and once the draft differs line 44 renders the parent's title struck beneath (`.prose-struck-beneath`). The description does not repeat: in the developing mode its read-only `<p class="headnote__prose">` is replaced by the textarea. Measured, the same at every width and both recipes: the h1 is 32px and 36 tall (the parent's title, static); the input is 32px, bold, boxed (the `.ink-field` hairline), 49 tall, with the width of the Sheet column (353 at 393, 584 at 744, 640 at 1024, 600 at 1366, 834 at 1600); once the title is changed three title lines stand on the page (the parent's in the h1, the new one in the field, the parent's again struck beneath); and the input is one line, so a longer title is clipped and scrolls inside the field (a 64-character test title overflows, `scrollWidth` greater than `clientWidth`, in all 10 changed readings; the 28-character "Olive Oil Ice Cream, lighter" is already clipped at 393, the board shows "Olive Oil Ice Cream, l"). The headnote of Olive Oil v1 is 315 tall unchanged and 336 changed at 393, 273 and 294 at 744 and up. The file's own header comment says the pen "transforms this block into the child's Sheet identity instead of leaving the parent's above a second version form", which the h1 contradicts.
**What the authority and the briefs say.** The hand-drawn `1600-pen.html` of 2026-09-24 (the file as it stood before the 2026-10-03 redraw, git `4494c95^`) draws the Sheet's header in the pen as the fields alone: a labelled "Sheet title" input (32px bold, pen blue, hairline box) then "Sheet description" and the helper line, and no heading above them. It never drew an h1 and a field. `route-recipe-version.md` § 3 says only that the version owns Sheet title and Sheet description and "the pen edits them with the rest of the Sheet"; `03.5-CONTEXT.md` D-09 seeds the values and says nothing of the pen's layout. So the pair is the app's defect, not the sketch's. **My own miss:** the 2026-10-03 redraw of `1600-pen.html` and the wide boards was generated from the built app and replaced the hand-drawn authority, so it carried the defect over; I should have compared the pen's front matter with the board it replaced. The redrawn `1600-pen` draws the h1 and the field.
**Two options on `sheet-title-pen.html`** (new board, the built app's own markup with the title's markup and one rule moved per row; rows: as built, A, B; columns: 393 unchanged, changed and a long title, then 1366 and 1600 unchanged and changed, cropped to the Sheet's front matter):
- **A, the field replaces the heading (recommended).** One field, set as the Sheet heading (2rem, bold, pen blue) and built like the description: no box (the description's `.prose-field` treatment), the small caption "Sheet title" above, the old title struck beneath only when it differs, the helper line once under the description as now. It is a textarea that grows (the app already gives every textarea `field-sizing: content`, app.css 296), so a long title wraps instead of clipping: at 393 the long title is four lines, 382 tall against 336 today. Measured on the board: front matter 254 unchanged and 309 changed at 393 (61 and 27 shorter than today), 212 and 233 at 1366 and 1600 (61 shorter at every width); the field is 44 tall for one line at 2rem (the touch floor) and grows from there.
- **B, the heading stays, Edit title turns it into the field.** The h1 shows the draft title (live), the old title struck beneath when they differ, and a 44px "Edit title" control under it swaps the heading for A's field. Front matter 274 and 331 at 393, 232 and 253 at 1366 and 1600 (20 taller than A). The pen shows no title field until asked, and the title costs one more tap.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): A.** It is what Mark asked for and what the hand-drawn authority drew; the title appears once (twice with the struck baseline, not three times); it saves 61px of a pen that is already long on the phone; and it makes title and description the same kind of control. B's one advantage is a heading that looks like the finished Sheet while the maker works on the ingredients; against it, one more tap, a second link word next to the band's "Rename" (the recipe's name, a different thing), and a heading that looks live but is not. **Details A must settle:** *touch and zoom:* 32px is far above the 16px that stops iOS from zooming a focused field, and one line is 44 tall (measured). *Focus:* the global `:focus-visible` ring, as the description gets; with no box, the ring and the pen blue are the only cues at rest, as for the description. If Mark wants a box at rest on both fields (the hand-drawn board had one on both), that is a one-rule change for both. *Long titles:* the textarea wraps; Enter must not add a line (a key handler, and newlines stripped on paste), so the title stays one line of text. *Helper line:* unchanged, under the description, naming both fields. *Reading view and print:* the h1 stays in the reading view and in print, unchanged; only the pen's developing mode drops it. *Accessibility:* the pen has no Sheet h1 while developing; the field's label "Sheet title" names it and the reading view restores the heading on save or Cancel.
**What it touches:** no approved decision (the pen's front matter was never decided in the sketch beyond the 1600-pen authority above); decision 26 and the pen boards are unaffected. **Redrawn if adopted:** `1600-pen` (its Olive Oil v1 and Mexican Chocolate v3 panels). **App files (not edited):** `app/src/ui/Headnote.jsx` (drop the h1 in the developing mode; the input becomes a textarea with the `prose-field` class and a newline guard), `app/src/ui/Headnote.test.jsx` (the cases at lines 81 and 148 and the markup the developing mode expects), `app/src/styles/app.css` (the `.headnote__sheet-title-field` rules at 496 to 505: the textarea takes the heading face, size and weight, and loses the top margin), and whatever pins the h1 and field pair in `RecipePage.test.jsx`. It is brief task 10 in decision 33's split, independent of the others.
**Questions for Mark:** (1) A or B? (2) With A, no box at rest like the description (as drawn), or a hairline box on both the title and the description? (3) If A, keep the caption "Sheet title" above the field (as drawn)? **Mark's answers (2026-10-04):** (1) A, one field replaces the heading. (2) No box at rest. (3) Keep the "Sheet title" caption. **Mark's later answer (2026-10-04, after quick 261004-igr):** the title field keeps the existing prose-field floor (48 with a mouse); no title-specific minimum height.

36. **"Before you start" as its own Sheet section, outside Instructions (Sid, 2026-10-04; Mark, 2026-10-03: "promote 'Before you start' into a separate section, outside Instructions", and on the todo, "I want to schedule this before Phase 04 b/c it impacts the printed page"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is approved).** Todo: `.planning/todos/pending/2026-10-03-promote-before-you-start-into-its-own-section-outside-instru.md`. Promoting it reverses what every document says today (the print brief line 48, `route-recipe.md` line 27, DESIGN.md lines 213 and 461, Phase 4 D-06), so it is a sketch decision first.
**What the app draws today (measured in the built app, WebKit, coarse pointer, the dist of 2026-10-04; `bys-capture.mjs`, `bys-measure.mjs`, `bys-measure.json`).** `app/src/ui/Method.jsx` renders `<h2 class="region-name">Instructions</h2>`, then a `div.method__before` holding `<p class="region-name">Before you start</p>` and the notes list, closed by a graduation-weight hairline (`app.css` 1035), then the steps; `RecipePage.jsx` wraps all of it in `section.method-region` (grid area `method`) and draws it when `showsMethodRegion` is true (steps or notes, or the pen; quick 261003-9bz). The label is a paragraph styled exactly like the heading above it, so on the page the two read as two headings of one weight. A version with steps and no notes (Mexican Chocolate v3, Strawberry v1) still renders the label, an empty list and the rule: 46px at 393. A version with notes and no steps draws the Instructions heading over the notes. Standard Base v2 (no steps, no notes) draws no region and takes the `recipe-page--no-method` class. In the pen the notes are editable fields with remove; the pen has no add-note control. The Sheet grid is `band / ingredients side / method side / foot` from 984 and `band / ingredients / side / method / foot` below it, so on the phone Balance and Watch for sit between the Ingredients and Instructions.
**What the authority says.** Nothing drawn before decision 36 separates the two: sketch 011 decision 6 keeps "Instructions, Before you start" open together, decision 10 keeps the inherited-note marker on Before you start notes, decision 11 names the steps region Instructions. The canvas's print drafts (`Method.dc.html`, "Bench sheet · page 4") head the Method page with Before you start inside it; Phase 4 D-06 says it prints once at the head of the method. D-07a puts the batch log first, so the formula page is page 3 and the Instructions page page 4.
**What is drawn** (five boards; the built app's own markup cropped to the Sheet, the app's stylesheets resolved at each width, one rule or one block of markup moved per row; generator `.planning/canvas-generators/bys.py` with `bys-capture.mjs` and `bys-measure.mjs`; WebKit only, nothing measured on the iPad):
- **Placement, `before-you-start-position.html`.** *A (recommended):* the section is a `section.before-region` with an `h2.region-name`, in its own grid row directly above Instructions: in the Sheet column it sits under the Ingredients (the order of the briefs: Sheet title, description, ingredients, Before you start, Instructions); on the phone it follows Balance and Watch for, so nothing that exists today moves. One rule. Sheet height 2,362 as built, 2,353 here at 1366; 2,766 and 2,745 at 393 (the old block's rule and padding go). *A1:* the same from 984 up; at 393 it comes ahead of Balance and Watch for, which then stand between it and Instructions. *B:* above the Ingredients, under the Sheet description (the order of doing); the table is 189px lower at 393 (top 801 to 990), and the Balance column starts level with the section at 1366.
- **States, `before-you-start-states.html`.** The section is drawn only when it has notes. Steps and no notes: no label, no empty list, no rule (393: the Sheet is 1,089 as built and 1,023 here; at 1366 the taller Balance column governs the height, so the page does not change). Notes and no steps: only Before you start renders; the Instructions section and its grid row leave (393: 1,525 and 1,459). Neither: nothing, no extra row gap. The inherited-note marker (constructed) stays after the note's text in small print.
- **Heading, `before-you-start-heading.html`.** *H1 (recommended):* the h2 alone, as Ingredients and Instructions; the gap between sections separates it; the old hairline is not carried over. *H2:* the h2 and the hairline (padding gap-m, then the section gap); 21px taller than H1 at both widths. Either way the label becomes a real heading (Phase 4 D-20 wants the region names as headings).
- **Pen, `before-you-start-pen.html`.** With notes: the editable fields and remove stay in the new section; Instructions keeps its heading and steps. With no notes: *E2 (recommended)* the heading alone, as Mark keeps the empty Instructions heading in the pen (2026-10-03) so two empty headings do not get two treatments; *E1* the section left out until an add-note control exists (393: the Sheet is 1,665 as built, 1,644 with E2, 1,599 with E1). With no steps and no notes: the pen keeps the Instructions heading, and with E2 the Before you start heading above it. The reading view draws nothing for no notes in every option.
- **Print, `before-you-start-print.html`.** The built page at letter width (816 by 1056, 56px side margins), the batch layer removed, the hand's italic fallback and black ink, no Phase 4 geometry (no footer, tick boxes, ruled As made column or step-group page rules; Phase 4 draws those). *PA (recommended):* the section opens the Instructions page, its own heading above the Instructions heading, a forced break before it; the formula page is unchanged; D-06 (once, at the head of the method, never repeated) holds. *PB:* it closes the formula page under the table, the Instructions page opening at the Instructions heading, and it moves whole to PA's place if it does not fit. *PC:* it opens the formula page above the table. **Measured fit, Olive Oil v1 (the only seeded version with notes):** the formula page's content is 873px of 968 usable, 95px to spare; the section needs 177px, so under PB it falls off the page by 82px, and under PC the table's last row crosses the page foot. So PB is PA for Olive Oil v1, and Phase 4's tick boxes and footer take more room, so PB would be PA for any version with a formula this long. Standard Base v2 (constructed with the two seeded notes) is the case where PB fits (543px, 425 to spare). With no notes nothing prints for the section; with notes and no steps the sheet ends with Before you start and there is no page 4.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): A, H1, E2, PA.** A is one rule, keeps the briefs' order, moves nothing that exists, and puts the notes where a maker looks next after weighing; B costs the table 189px on the phone for a section that is empty on most versions; A1 separates the notes from Instructions by two screen-only folds. H1 matches every other Sheet heading; H2 adds a form no peer has. E2 is consistency with Mark's own pen decision. PA is the only print placement that holds for the version that has notes, keeps D-06 and the brief's page sequence, and needs no fit rule.
**What it touches:** no approved decision is reversed. It amends decision 6's wording ("Instructions, Before you start" stay open: both stay open, as two sections) and replaces the print brief's sentence that Before you start "heads the Instructions", `route-recipe.md` line 27's parts list (the parts stay in the same order; only the grouping changes), DESIGN.md lines 213 and 461 (the Instructions-step entry's last sentence moves to a new Before-you-start entry), and Phase 4 D-06's "at the head of the method" (now "at the head of the Instructions page", same effect). Decisions 10, 11 and 18 are unaffected (the marker's home, the screen name, the folds); `route-recipe-version.md` line 63's marker and staleness reading read the same notes and are unaffected, but the pen's copy of notes into the draft is rechecked in the app task.
**App change brief (for `/gsd-quick` after Mark approves; nothing here edits `app/`).** (1) `RecipePage.jsx`: render `<section className="before-region" aria-label="Before you start"><h2 className="region-name">Before you start</h2><NoteList .../></section>` directly before the `method-region` section in DOM order (visual order comes from the grid areas), when the notes list is not empty (reading) or, with E2, in the pen. (2) `Method.jsx`: drop the `method__before` block and its props (`beforeYouStart`, `onChangeNoteText`, `onRemoveNote` move to the new section's caller); `showsMethodRegion` keys on steps (or the pen) only; add a `showsBeforeRegion`. (3) `app.css`: grid areas with a `before` row directly above `method` at both widths (the four combinations of before and method present, as `bys.py` `grid_css` writes them: wide `band band / ingredients side / before side / method side / foot foot`, narrow `band / ingredients / side / before / method / foot`), a `recipe-page--no-before` modifier next to `--no-method` (or `:has()`), `.before-region{grid-area:before;min-width:0;max-width:var(--measure-prose)}`, and the `.method__before` rule (line 1035) removed; the app's two `--no-method` rules (lines 442 and 2348) are replaced by the four-way set. (4) Tests: whatever pins `method__before`, the empty list and `recipe-page--no-method` (`RecipePage.test.jsx`, `cross-cutting.test.js`, the 261003-9bz cases). (5) Print: leave `@media print` alone for now; Phase 4 owns the print route and takes PA from this board. (6) Documents: the five lines above and Phase 4 `04-CONTEXT.md` D-06. (7) Verification: the five boards against the build in WebKit and Chrome, then the iPad.
**Found while drawing, said plainly.** (1) Where the Balance column is the taller one (Mexican Chocolate v3 at 1366), the Sheet column's rows stretch and leave a gap above the first section: 399px above Instructions as built. That is existing and not part of this change, but removing the empty label block makes it 33px larger (432px). A `1fr` last row would put the gap at the foot; that changes a look nobody asked about, so it is not drawn. (2) The print boards show the built page, not a print design: the fit numbers are the best case. (3) The reading and pen boards use Olive Oil v1 with its batch in view, so step 1 reads struck ("Skipped") in the hand; that is the real page, not the proposal. (4) Not verified on the iPad (WebKit on a Mac only).
**Questions for Mark:** (1) Placement: A, A1 or B? (2) Heading: H1 or H2? (3) Pen with no notes: E2 (heading kept) or E1 (left out)? (4) Print: PA (opens the Instructions page), PB (closes the formula page, falling back to PA) or PC? (5) Does "outside Instructions" also mean the reading view's Instructions heading leaves when there are no steps, as drawn (it already does since 261003-9bz)? **Mark's answers (2026-10-04):** (1) Placement B, above the Ingredients under the description, with his reasoning that Before you start, Ingredients, Instructions is a timeline for the cook (what to do ahead, what to gather, what to do). (2) Heading H1, the heading alone. (3) Pen with no notes: E2, the heading kept alone. (4) Print PC, opens the formula page above the table. (5) Yes: the Instructions heading leaves the reading view when there are no steps. **Consequences Sid measured, for the build:** B puts the table top 189px lower at 393; PC does not fit one page for Olive Oil v1 (the table's last row crosses the page foot, best case before Phase 04's footer and tick boxes), so PC needs a fit rule or a decision on where the overflow goes. **Mark's later answer (2026-10-04) on print overflow:** always PC. If the ingredients table does not fit on one page, break it by steps, so that the ingredients grouped into a step all print on the same page, and repeat the table header on the next page. Sid draws it; no build until then. **Drawn 2026-10-04: decision 45** (board `before-you-start-print-break.html`).

37. **The Why row in the version details (Sid, 2026-10-04; Mark, 2026-09-27: "add a note to look into the Why row"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is approved).** Todo: `.planning/todos/pending/2026-09-27-why-row-in-version-details.md`. Board `why-row.html`; generators `.planning/canvas-generators/why.py`, `why-capture.mjs`, `why-measure.mjs`.
**What the app draws today (measured in the built app, WebKit and Chromium, the dist of 2026-10-04, at 1600 fine pointer, 1366 fine and coarse, 393 coarse; Mexican Chocolate v3 with its seeded Why, v2 and Olive Oil v1 with none).** The details are a two-track grid (`.notebook-version__details`: a `max-content` label track, a 14px gap, a value track). Written, From version and From batch put the label in track one and the value in track two (`.versions__lineage`, which also resets the dd's margin, `app.css` 591). The Why label and value both span both tracks (`grid-column: 1 / -1`, `app.css` 628), so the value sits under the label, and it starts **40px** right of the label's left edge at every width and in both engines, with a saved Why and with none. The 40px is the browser's default start margin on a `dd`: `.version-row__reason` is not in the `.versions__lineage` rule that resets it. The todo's "about 58px" is the width of the label track when there is no From version row (58.25px); the measured indent is 40. The label track is 97px with From version and 58px without, so Written's value sits at 111 or 72. The saved Why is set in the text face, not the hand: `VersionRow.jsx` gives the dd `.prose-text` (Georgia 16px, leading 24, sheet pen blue, the note role), 72px tall (3 lines) in the live 1366 band and 96px (4 lines) at 393; the empty value is the grotesk at 13px in ink, 18.2px tall. The Hand entry (DESIGN.md line 315), the Hand Rule (line 325) and decision 17 all say the saved Why shows in the hand; neither the app nor any board drew it (`1600-batch.html` and `1366-batch.html` are the app's own markup and carry the same 40px and the same Georgia). DESIGN.md line 466 says Why "spans both tracks and reads as authored prose at the note role", which is what is built apart from the indent. The older hand-drawn authority (`details-fold.html`, `gen.py` line 146) drew Why inline in track two with margin 0, in secondary grey, for the empty case only; a saved Why was never drawn on any board before this one.
**What is drawn** (one board, the built app's own markup cropped to the Version section, the app's stylesheets resolved at each width, one block of CSS and at most one class per column; WebKit only, nothing measured on the iPad; each panel's caption carries its measured left edges and heights).
- *As built:* the 40px indent.
- *A, inline like Written:* the label in track one, the value in track two, first baselines level, the saved Why in the hand (`.app-hand`: Caveat 22px, leading 1.25, sheet pen blue). Two rules.
- *B, its own line, flush with the label (recommended):* the grid as built, the dd's start margin reset to 0, the saved Why in the hand. One declaration. The value's left edge is the label's (0) at every width and for both label-track widths.
- *B in the text face:* B with the text face the app draws today, to show the face is a separate question from the layout.
**Measured on the board (WebKit, the final-design band: version column 553 at 1600, 549 at 1366, 353 at 393).** Saved Why, Version section height: as built 290, A 269.5, B 300.5, B in the text face 266 at 1600 and 1366; 314, 352, 328 and 314 at 393. The hand Why is 82.5px (3 lines) in B and in A at 1600 and 1366; at 393 it is 110px (4 lines) in B and 165px (6 lines) in A, where the value track is 242 wide. With no Why, A is 31px shorter than as built or B at every width (205 against 236 with From version, 184 against 215 without), because the value joins the label's line.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): B, with the saved Why in the hand.** B is one declaration; it keeps the structure DESIGN.md line 466 already describes (Why spans both tracks, authored prose, not a compact fact); the prose gets the whole column (4 lines against 6 at 393); and its left edge does not move with the label track (A's moves between 111 and 72 from one version to the next). A is the empty state's older drawing and reads as one grammar for every row; its cost is the narrow value track at 393 and a prose paragraph hung beside a 12px caption. Not drawn: the value on its own line hung at the value track (it would move 111 or 72 with the From row), and a mix (inline when empty, own line when saved: the label's row would change shape with state).
**What it touches:** no approved decision is reversed. B needs no DESIGN.md edit (line 466 already says it). A would amend line 466. The hand needs none (lines 315 and 325 already say it); it closes a gap between the app and decision 17. `details-fold.html` and `gen.py` line 146 (the hand-drawn empty state, inline) become stale under B.
**App change brief (for `/gsd-quick` after Mark approves; nothing here edits `app/`).** (1) `app.css` `.version-row__reason` (line 637): add `margin-inline-start: 0` (B). For A instead: `.version-row__reason-label {grid-column: 1; margin-top: 0; align-self: baseline}` and `.version-row__reason {grid-column: 2; margin: 0; align-self: baseline}`. (2) If the hand: `VersionRow.jsx` (the dd near line 363) takes `app-hand` in place of `prose-text` for a saved Why; the `--empty` variant is unchanged. `.version-row__reason` sets `font-size: var(--sheet-type-note)` after `.app-hand` and ties on specificity, so the dd would stay 16px: the board's rule is `.version-row__reason.app-hand {font-size: max(var(--size-hand), var(--size-hand-min)); line-height: var(--leading-hand)}`. (3) Tests: `VersionRow.test.jsx` line 381 pins `version-row__reason prose-text`. (4) Verification: the board against the build at 1600, 1366 and 393 in WebKit and Chromium, then the iPad.

38. **The App-context radius, against the rail's active place (Sid, 2026-10-04; Mark ruled at 03.4 UAT on 2026-09-22 to keep the square block as shipped until an App radius is settled; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is approved).** Todo: `.planning/todos/pending/2026-09-22-settle-app-context-radius-then-revisit-rail-active-item.md`. Board `app-radius.html`; generators `.planning/canvas-generators/radius.py`, `radius-capture.mjs`, `radius-measure.mjs`.
**What exists (measured in the built app, WebKit, 1600 fine pointer, and read from `tokens.css`, `shell.css`, `home.css`, `notebook.css`).** The App already carries five radius tokens: 3px on the sprinkle marks (`--app-radius-sprinkle`), 4px on the row marks (`--app-radius-rail`), 8px on the ceremony field (`--app-notebook-field-radius`), and 10px on the filled and outline actions (`--app-radius-action`; `home.css` 302, `notebook.css` 251 and 269) and the Home lead block (`--app-radius-lead`, `home.css` 80); History's marks are circles. Nothing else has a radius: the rail's places, the menu button, the tools and the tab row are square. A rail place is 183 by 44 at x 20 (padding 12 and 20, 14px, weight 400); the active place is the subtle surface (#f3f4f2, 1.10:1 against white) in its destination's text colour, no radius, no weight. At 393 the active tab is a 79 by 55 subtle block, edge to edge. Board 170 (`AltResumeSideNav`) draws every place at 8px, the active place on the subtle surface in weight 600, rows 38 high at 15px; its "pill" is 8px on a 38px row, not a pill. Board 171 (`Phone390Tabs`) draws the active tab in weight 600 with no surface.
**What the documents say.** DESIGN.md line 405: rounded controls are the approved direction, "no exact radius or component shape is established"; line 411: "there is no radius token" (the Sheet has none; the App has five); line 495 (Navigation): "The active place underlines in its own colour" (approved direction, not implemented), while the shell draws a subtle surface (`shell.css`, D-09) and board 170 a surface at 8px. The active place is described three ways.
**What is drawn** (the built app's own markup, the app's stylesheets including `home.css`; each row moves the radius tokens and one rule on the rail's places; panels: the rail with Notebook active and with Home active, the Next version pen's field and its two actions, the Home lead block and its two actions).
- *As shipped:* places square; field 8; actions and lead block 10.
- *A, one radius, 8px:* the places take board 170's 8; the field stays 8; the actions and the lead block move from 10 to 8 (two tokens, four rules).
- *B, one radius, 10px (recommended):* the actions and the lead block stay 10; the field moves from 8 to 10 (one token, one rule); the places take 10 (board 170 draws 8).
- *C, two named values (8 for rows and fields, 10 for actions and the lead block):* nothing built moves; the places take board 170's 8. It is a split, so by the house rule it needs a named exception, and the field and the filled action are the same height and kind of thing.
- *Weight and focus ring, drawn on B:* the active place at 600 as boards 170 and 171 draw it (the app draws 400); the radius on every place, so the 2px focus ring on a place follows its shape, against the radius on the active place only, where a focused place keeps a square ring.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): B, with the radius on every rail place and the active place in weight 600.** One value app-wide is the house rule, and B moves the fewest built things; 8 against 10 is a 2px difference on 44px items, hard to see on the board. The subtle surface is 1.10:1 against white, so the current place leans on its colour alone; DESIGN.md line 390 asks for discernible state without relying on colour, and both boards 170 and 171 give the active place weight 600. The radius on every place (not only the active one) keeps the focus ring one shape down the rail.
**What DESIGN.md would say (not edited; Mark decides).** *Shapes, "App interface" (line 405):* replace "but no exact radius or component shape is established" with: "App controls, the rail's places and the lead block share one radius, 10px (`--app-radius-control`). The marks keep their own: 3px on a sprinkle, 4px on a row's mark, circles on History's. The Sheet stays square; the radius applies to App interactive elements and the surfaces they sit on, never to the Sheet's tables, rules, chips or fields, or to anything that prints." *Line 411:* "there is no radius token" becomes "the Sheet has no radius token; the App's is `--app-radius-control`", and "a builder who wants one adds it to `tokens.css` first" goes. *App marks, Navigation (line 495):* "The active place underlines in its own colour" becomes "The active place sits on the subtle surface with the shared radius, in weight 600 and its destination's colour; there is no underline and no colour bar", with "Home underlines in app blue" becoming "Home takes app blue". Tabs inside a recipe keep their line.
**App change brief (for `/gsd-quick` after Mark approves; nothing here edits `app/`).** (1) `tokens.css`: one `--app-radius-control: 10px` in place of `--app-radius-action`, `--app-radius-lead` and `--app-notebook-field-radius`, consumers at `home.css` 80 and 302 and `notebook.css` 229, 251 and 269 (or keep the three names at one value; the house rule asks for one). (2) `shell.css`: `.shell__place {border-radius: var(--app-radius-control)}` and `.shell__place[aria-current='page'] {font-weight: 600}`; `shell.test.js` pins the file's selectors. (3) The tab row is not drawn here: board 171 has the active tab bold with no surface, the shipped tab is a 79 by 55 subtle block; that is its own call. (4) Verification: the board against the build side by side in WebKit and Chromium, then the iPad.
**Found while drawing, said plainly.** (1) DESIGN.md line 495 describes an underline the shell never drew. (2) The shipped tab row and board 171 disagree (surface against weight). (3) The built shell is still the one before decision 33 (the rail from 984, no sticky header); the rail panels are the rail as built, which decision 33 keeps from 1590, and the fly-out reuses the same `.shell__place` rule. (4) 1600 fine pointer only; not verified on the iPad.
**Questions for Mark:** (1) Radius: A, B or C? (2) The active place in weight 600? (3) Does the tab row follow, and as which of its two drawings? **Mark's answers (2026-10-04):** (1) Radius B, one 10px radius. (2) The active rail place takes weight 600. (3) The phone tab row follows the shipped surface block, with the radius. The DESIGN.md edit described above is not made; it is an /impeccable document pass. **Mark's later answer (2026-10-04):** the phone tab row's active tab also takes weight 600, like the rail.

39. **The rhythm of the Version details (Sid, 2026-10-04; Mark, on a todo: "Sid needs to review the rhythm of the Version section details. there is more vertical space between From Version and Why than there is between Written and From Version"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is approved).** Todo: `.planning/todos/pending/2026-10-04-sid-needs-to-review-the-rhythm-of-the-version-section-detail.md`. Board `version-details-rhythm.html`; generators `.planning/canvas-generators/rhythm.py`, `rhythm-capture.mjs`, `rhythm-measure.mjs`.
**What the app draws today (measured in the built app after quick 261004-ly6, the dists of 2026-10-04 16:34 and 16:38, WebKit and Chromium (the installed Chrome), at 1600 fine pointer, 1366 coarse and 393 coarse; Mexican Chocolate v3 with its saved Why, v2 with none and a From version, Olive Oil v1 with none and no From version; the preview server already on :4173, nothing rebuilt here; the quick-batch rebuilt the dist between the two readings and all 18 readings per engine's set were identical; the two engines agree to 1px).** The gap between one line and the next, from the bottom of a line's box to the top of the next one's, is the same at every width, in both engines, with a saved Why and with none: **Written to From version 4px; From version to Why 16px; Why's label to its words 4px.** Without a From version (a first version) it is Written to Why 16px. As baseline pitch that is 21px from Written to From version and 33px from From version to Why. The label track (97px with From version, 58px without) and the pointer change none of it.
**Where the extra comes from: one rule.** `.notebook-version__details` is a grid with a 4px row gap (`--app-notebook-gap-hairline`, `notebook.css` 409). Written and From version each put a label and a value on one row, so they sit 4px apart. The Why label opens its own row and carries `margin-top: var(--gap-s)` (12px, `app.css` 636), added to the 4px row gap: 16px. The file's own comment says why: the margin "opens a new line in this stack", the same one the History control uses; it was written when Why was a prose block set apart from the facts. Quick 261004-ly6 made the value flush and in the hand and left this margin as it was. Nothing else adds space: the labels' line boxes are 15 to 17px, the values' margin is 2px on the facts and 0 on Why, and no padding is set on any row. After the saved words the next row (From batch, drawn only when a version cites a batch, none is seeded) sits 4px under them, so as built Why has 16px above and 4px below: the break is not a group break either.
**What the eye sees (a 2x screenshot of each panel, the gap from the lowest ink of one line to the highest of the next; a link's underline counts).** As built: 9.5px between Written and From version, 19.5px between From version and Why, 13px between the Why label and the saved words (the hand's own top space sits under the label: its line box is 27.5px for 22px Caveat). So the box numbers understate the label-to-words gap by about 9px, and a single box gap of 4px (option A) would put the Why label 7.5px under From version and 13px above its words.
**What is drawn** (one board, the built app's own markup cropped to the Version section; the band at the final design's width; one or two rules moved per column; every caption carries its measured box and seen gaps and the section's height; WebKit only, nothing measured on the iPad). Rows: a saved Why, no Why, and a first version, at 1600, 1366 and 393, then a constructed From batch row at 1366.
- *As built:* 4 / 16 / 4 (box). Section 300.5 tall with a saved Why at 1600 and 1366, 328 at 393.
- *A, one 4px gap:* the Why label loses its top margin (`margin-top: 0`); one declaration. Box 4 / 4 / 4; seen 9.5 / 7.5 / 13. Section 12px shorter than built. The label then sits nearer the line above (7.5) than its own words (13), so it reads as part of the From version line.
- *B, one 8px gap, the label tucked to its words (recommended):* the details' row gap becomes 8px, the Why label has no top margin and a 4px negative bottom margin, so it keeps 4px to its words. Box 8 / 8 / 4; seen 13.5 / 11.5 / 13 with a saved Why and with From batch 12 after it. The three gaps Mark compared read within 2px of each other; the section is 4px shorter than built (296.5 against 300.5); the first-version case reads 14 / 11.
- *C, the facts untouched:* Written and From version stay at 4; the Why label keeps 2px of top margin (`--gap-hair`) and sits on its words (negative bottom margin 4px). Box 4 / 6 / 0; seen 9.5 / 9.5 / 9. Even to the eye, but the label touches the hand's ascenders and the block reads cramped. Section 14px shorter than built.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): B.** It is the only option where the three gaps Mark compared are equal to the eye (13.5, 11.5 and 13px seen, against 9.5, 19.5 and 13 as built), it keeps the Why label nearer its words than a tight list would, and it costs the facts 4px each, which is looser, not tighter, so nothing reads cramped. A is one declaration but leaves the label hugging From version. C is the smallest change to the facts and the tightest result. Not drawn: keeping a 12px break and moving From batch above Why (the facts together, Why last); it leaves Mark's complaint as it is when no batch is cited.
**What it touches:** no approved decision is reversed; decision 37 (the Why value's place and face) is untouched. B's 8px has no token (the spacing scale is 2, 4 as `--app-notebook-gap-hairline`, 6, 12, 20): it is twice `--app-notebook-gap-hairline`, or a new App token for the details' row gap; the house rule asks for one value, so the second consumer (the pen's own label-to-control gap, which reads the 4px token) stays on the 4px token and the details get their own name.
**App change brief (for `/gsd-quick` after Mark approves; nothing here edits `app/`).** (1) `notebook.css` `.notebook-version__details`: row-gap from `var(--app-notebook-gap-hairline)` to the 8px value (B). (2) `app.css` `.version-row__reason-label`: `margin-top: var(--gap-s)` removed; add `margin-bottom: -4px` (the label's 4px to its words; B), written as a negative of the 4px token. (3) `app.css` comment at 656 (`.version-row__history` "the same top margin .version-row__reason-label uses") rewritten: the History control keeps `margin-top: var(--gap-s)` and no longer shares it. (4) Tests: whatever pins `version-row__reason-label`'s margin or the details' row gap (`VersionRow.test.jsx`, `notebook.test.js`). (5) Verification: the board against the build in WebKit and Chromium at 1600, 1366 and 393, then the iPad.
**Found while drawing, said plainly.** (1) The live 1366 band is the old layout (the details list is 451 wide), the board draws decision 33's (549); the gaps are the same in both. (2) The negative margin is a workaround for dt and dd being separate grid rows; the clean form would put the Why label and its words in one grid item, a markup change in `VersionRow.jsx` that this decision does not need. (3) Written to From version moves from 4 to 8 under B: Mark did not ask for it, it is how the rows become even. (4) Not verified on the iPad; no seeded version cites a batch, so the From batch panel is constructed from the app's own row markup. (5) `cssscope.py` (the generators' stylesheet scoper) now drops shell.css's one `html` rule, which the sticky header (quick 261004-ly8) added and a panel cannot scope; no board output changes.
**Questions for Mark:** (1) B, A, C or leave as built? (2) With B, a new App token for the details' row gap (recommended) or twice the 4px token? **Mark's answers (2026-10-04):** (1) B, one 8px gap with the Why label tucked to its words. (2) A new App token for the details' row gap.

40. **The record pen in App context, redrawn on today's build, and its group cues (Sid, 2026-10-04; todo 2026-09-25 "Draw the App-context record pen"; the For Sid note of 2026-10-01 on section rhythm; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is approved).** Todo: `.planning/todos/pending/2026-09-25-draw-the-record-pen-in-app-context.md`. **The todo is stale on one point, said first.** It says sketch 011 never drew the recording state. Decision 29 (2026-10-02, approved: "app-context pen is good") drew it at 350 and 393 (`350-pen-app.html`, `393-pen-app.html`). What is true is that it was never built: the app's `.notebook-log` still wears the Sheet's square ink boxes and pen-blue fill (no `border-radius` on a stop, segment, field or action in the record pen; `notebook.css` and `app.css` carry none of decision 29's `SKIN`), and no plan was ever written against the drawing. Three things since change its basis: decision 38 retired `--app-notebook-field-radius` and `--app-radius-action` for one `--app-radius-control` (10px), which the skin read; quick 261004-igr built the dot-separated labels (the batch head and the Tasting row); and the log column is 350 at 1366, not the 300 and 340 the todo names.
**What is drawn.** (1) `record-pen-app.html`: the built app's own log markup (WebKit, coarse pointer, the dist of 2026-10-04 after quick 261004-ly7, Olive Oil v1; `recordpen-capture.json`) with the app's own stylesheets resolved at the window, in three states at the 350 column (a 1366 window) and at 393: *Reading* (the batch as it reads, observations in the hand), *Recording, the pen opens* (from Record another, the tasting not yet added: Add tasting, Cancel, Save batch) and *Recording, filled* (every field typed, the tasting added, stops, segments and two defects picked). The only addition is decision 29's skin, now on the one radius: text fields and joined stops and segments at `--app-radius-control` (10px outer corners), the picked fill in the filled action's colour (`--app-blue-text`, white label), boundaries on stops at `--app-text-secondary`, defect squares at the row-mark 4px, Cancel the outline action and Save batch the filled one, the page's own rules in the divider colour; the maker's notes stay pen blue in the text face (decision 17's Hand Rule: typed in the prose-field role, shown in the hand once saved, which the reading panel shows). No size, gap or face moves. **Conformance, measured:** each panel's height equals the live app's to 0.1px (350: 898.7, 767.3 and 2483.4; 393: 556.2, 789.7 and 2612.2) and no panel has content past its column edge (0px at both widths). So decision 28's limits hold for the drawn pen exactly as for the built one: at 350 the stacked arrangement has the 134px to spare it had. (2) `record-pen-cues.html`: the same filled pen cropped from the first cue to the end of the defects, with the For Sid note's open question.
**What the cues do today (measured in the built app, WebKit coarse; 350 and 393).** "Every recipe", "This recipe only" and, in the defects, the same two cues, are `.pen-caption.axes-cue`: 12px, 500, small caps, ink, the same face as the axis names and field labels they head ("Hardness", "Churn date"), and "Any problems?" is that face again. Their spacing is worse than their face: the first cue sits **60px above its first axis name at 350 and 75px at 393** (the cue's own 6px margin, the grid's 20px gap and the axis mark's own 20px top margin, then the 44px axis head with its name at the bottom), while the labels sit about 40px from each other and the previous group's last item ends only **33px** above the next cue (the stacked flex gap 20, the declared group's 12px padding and its rule). So the cue is nearer the group before it than the items it heads, and every axis name reads as the next one in the list. The defects are tighter (cue to chips 6px; "Any problems?" to its cue 26px), so the axes and the defects group their cues differently too.
**Options, one or two rules per column (`record-pen-cues.html`; the first cue to the last defect group, 350 and 393).**
- *A, the cue's face:* 14px, 600, sentence case, ink (`--app-size-meta`); no spacing changes. The heading reads as a heading; the 60 and 75px stay, so it still floats between the groups. Region 15px taller than built.
- *B, the cue's space:* 32px between groups (`--gap-l`), 12px from the cue to its first item's box (the axis mark's own 20px top margin dropped on the first item; at 393, 15px more because the 44px head puts the name low). Seen gaps: cue to its first label 26px at both widths (60 and 75 built), last item to the next cue 45px (33 built), "Any problems?" to its cue 12px (26 built), cue to chips 6px (unchanged). Region 46px shorter at 350 and 62px at 393 (1131.7 against 1177.7; 1206.6 against 1268.9). The cue is now nearer its items than the group before it, but it still reads in the labels' face.
- *C, both (recommended):* A's face and B's space. Region 31px shorter at 350 and 55px at 393 than built. The cue is a heading in its own face, 26px above its first label and 45px below the previous group's last one.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): C.** It fixes both halves of what the note says (face and spacing) with the least decoration: no rule, tint or indent is added to a 350px column, and the cue still disappears cleanly if "This recipe only" is empty (SEED-261001-iq4: no rule or surface is left behind; the declared group's existing hairline is the one that goes with it). B alone leaves the cue reading as one more label; A alone leaves it floating. Not drawn: a tinted block per group (a subtle surface, 10px radius; the column has 134px to spare but the surface costs 24px of the label width) and a rail down the group's left edge; both add decoration the pen does not have.
**What the skin touches:** no approved decision is reversed. It builds decision 29 as approved, on decision 38's token. The Sheet's foot (`PenFoot.jsx`: Cancel, Save and Add tasting at the end of the Sheet) stays in the Sheet's square grammar, as decision 29 flagged: it is the same `.save-ceremony` component as the log's own, so the two feet differ in look; unchanged here. The 1.4:1 text-field hairline (decision 29 flag 1) is unchanged: Mark looked and did not change it.

Batches is **constructed**: the app draws it only with two batches and none is seeded, so it is the Tasting row's markup with the Batches words ("Batches, Show, 3 batches"); Tasting with a date is constructed the same way (the seeded tasting has none).
**Measured.** As built the info sits far from the control word: History 187px at 393 and 517 at 723, Go to batch 192 and 522 (awaiting tasting), 244 and 574 (tasted), Tasting 128 and 458 (date unknown), 141 and 471 (dated), Batches 186 and 516, the batch head 308 at 723 (at 393 it already wraps). With A every row reads 32px from its control word to the info (the dot sits 14px after the word and the info 14px after the dot), at both widths. **Every row is still one button or link, full width, 44px tall** (353 and 683 wide): the whole row stays the tap target and the blank end of the row stays tappable; nothing about the button changes, the dot is CSS, the accessible names ("History, Show, 4 versions", "Go to batch, Awaiting tasting") are the build's. The batch head is 78.2px tall at 393 (the actions wrap under the date, as today: 18.2 plus the head's 16 plus 44) and 44 at 723, where the actions follow the date 32px later.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): as drawn, 32px at the phone too.** One value at every width (decision 34's rule), and it fits at 393 with room: the longest row, Tasting with "tasted date unknown", sits on one line well inside the 353px row (on the board). A smaller gap at the phone (20px) would be a split with no reason, since nothing is tight.
**What it touches:** decision 34 only (its answer 4, "every width"). **App change brief (for `/gsd-quick` after Mark approves; nothing here edits `app/`).** (1) `notebook.css`: the 1057 block loses its `(min-width: 724px)` prelude for the rules that read the same at every width (`.notebook .fold-row`, the two `::before` rules, `.notebook-jump`), or the block is moved out of the media query; the batch head's `justify-content: flex-start; column-gap` too (decision 42). (2) Tests: `notebook.test.js` pins the block's cut at 724; its cases change. (3) The phone's boards `393-batch.html`, `723-batch.html`, `393-all-folded.html`, `393-phone-log.html`, `723-phone-log.html` and the count boards carry the old far-end rows and are not redrawn here. (4) Verification: the board against the build in WebKit and Chromium at 393 and 723, then the iPhone.
**Found while drawing, said plainly.** (1) At 393 the batch head is the same in A and as built: the actions already wrap under the date there, so at 393 the 32px has no effect (the date and the actions together, 406px, do not fit the 353px row); at 723 they fit and follow the date. (2) The seeded data has no Batches row, so it is constructed. (3) Not verified on the iPhone. (4) The Tasting row in the log is drawn closed, as the app opens it at 393; the log at 393 keeps it closed.
**Questions for Mark:** (1) The 32px gap at the phone as drawn, or a smaller one? (2) Redraw every other board that carries these rows (the wide boards `744-batch` to `1920-batch`, `1600-pen`, `1600-no-batch`, `1600-long-history`, the sticky-nav boards and the phone boards above) with the dots now, or only when you ask? Sid recommends now, so the boards match the build and the phone's build plan. **Mark's answers (2026-10-04):** (1) 32px at the phone, as at the wide widths. (2) Redraw the other boards with the dots now. **Drawn 2026-10-04: decision 46.**

42. **The batch head from 744 up keeps its height (Sid, 2026-10-04; Mark's later answer to decision 34: the batch head keeps its height at 1366 and up, and Sid redraws row A with a 16px gap between lines; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is approved).** Board `info-labels-batch-head.html` (the same generators as decision 41).
**What happened.** Row A of decision 34 set `gap: var(--gap-l)` on the batch head, which is both the gap between date and actions on one line and the gap between the two lines when the actions wrap. In the 350 log column the actions wrap, so the 16px between the lines became 32px and the head grew from 78.22 to 94.22px. Quick 261004-igr built it that way (`notebook.css` 1082).
**What is drawn** (the built app's own markup, Olive Oil v1; WebKit coarse; 1366 and 744). Three columns: before decision 34 (space between, the head's own 16px gap), A as built (32px both ways) and A redrawn: `column-gap: var(--gap-l)` and `row-gap: var(--app-notebook-log-head-outer-gap)` (16px). **Measured:** at 1366 and up in the 350 column the head is **78.22** before, **94.22** as built and **78.22** redrawn (the date line 18.2, the gap 16, the actions 44); the actions sit 16px under the date's line as before. At 744 to 1365, where the log is full width and the actions fit on the date's line, the head is 44px in all three and the gap from the date to the actions is 289px before, 32 as built and 32 redrawn: the redraw changes nothing there.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): the redraw.** It is Mark's answer, exactly: 32px where date and actions share a line, the head's own 16px where they wrap, the head's height unchanged. **App change brief:** `notebook.css` (the batch head rule at 1082): `gap: var(--gap-l)` becomes `column-gap: var(--gap-l)`; the base rule's `gap: var(--app-notebook-log-head-outer-gap)` then supplies the row gap; `notebook.test.js`'s case on the head's gap; the probe (`261004-igr-probe.mjs`) pins 94.22 and changes to 78.22. **Found:** the 32px is a column gap only, so at 393, where the actions already wrap, it has no effect (decision 41). **Questions for Mark:** none open: Mark's answer is the drawing; this is the look.

43. **The Go to batch row, start-aligned with a dot, in the wide band (Sid, 2026-10-04; Mark's answer to the decision 33 open item: "yes, dots like decision 34"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is approved).** Board `724-1365-go-to-batch.html`, redrawn in place (generator `final.py`, `nav-candidates.css` section JUMP).
