49. **The empty space under the ingredient table on Mexican Chocolate v3, and options to remove it (Sid, 2026-10-05; Mark, on the iPad, about the 325px found by quick 261004-oxa: "it bothers me. ask Sid to fix."; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is decided or approved).** Board `empty-space-under-table.html`; generators `.planning/canvas-generators/empty.py` (from `empty-capture.mjs`), `empty-measure.mjs` and `empty-probe.mjs` (the build with each option added; WebKit coarse, and Chrome for the probe, from the preview server on :4173, the dist of 21:00:30, nothing started or built); the check is `empty-conform.mjs`.
**What it is, measured.** The Sheet's grid (`.recipe-page`) is two columns from 984 with the areas `ingredients side` and `method side`: the side column (Balance, then Watch for, both open from 984) spans the table's row and the Instructions' row. On Mexican Chocolate v3 the side column is 1,433px tall at 984 and 1366 (Balance 979, Watch for 422, a 32px gap) and the table and the Instructions need about 700 between them, so the grid shares the 733px it lacks equally between the two rows. The Instructions therefore start **399px under the table's last line, 367px of it empty, at 984 and 1366** (WebKit, Show changes off, as the app opens): 352px at 1024, 300 at 1194, 285 at 1365, 360 at 1600, 307 at 1920. Chrome reads about 40px less (313px at 1024, 334 at 1366); the 325 in the quick's summary sits between. **It is not only 984 to 1365: it is every width from 984 up**, 1366 and the 1590 rail layout included, wherever the side column is taller than the table and the Instructions together. With Show changes on the table is taller and it is 213px at 1024; with the pen open about 180px. The same 367px stands under the Instructions, at the foot of the column. Olive Oil v1 has 17px under its table, because its Instructions are long (1,274px): the two rows already fill the side column's 2,076px, so the grid has no surplus to share. Underbelly Light Base v2 and Standard Base v2 have no Instructions, so their table's row is the only row and the column simply ends: 902 and 888px empty under the table at 1366, the column's foot. Decision 48's one-line head makes Mexican Chocolate v3's empty space 15px larger (the 31px the table loses is shared by the two rows): 383 at 984.
**A (recommended).** The Instructions' row takes all the side column's surplus: the grid's rows are `auto auto 1fr auto` (band, table, Instructions, foot; `auto 1fr auto` with no Instructions). A style rule only; measured in the build with the rule added (WebKit and Chrome agree): the Instructions start **49px under the table** at 984, 1024, 1366 and 1600, 17px of it empty (the same 49px as Olive Oil v1), page heights unchanged, and the Sheet's column ends where its Instructions end, **702px empty at 1024 and 732px at 984 and 1366**, beside the tail of Watch for (the foot every recipe without Instructions already has, but at 888px or more there). Olive Oil v1, Underbelly Light Base v2 and Standard Base v2 are unchanged; with Show changes on, with the pen open and below 984 it is the same effect or none. The cost: a large empty area at the foot of the left column, where there is now a short Instructions section beside a tall Balance column.
**AB.** A, and Watch for closed when the page opens (Balance stays open): the side column is 1,046px at 1024 and 1,061 at 1366, the page 357px shorter at 1024 (2,359 to 2,002; 2,316 to 1,944 at 1366) and the foot's empty space 345px at 1024 and 360 at 1366. Watch for is one tap away. This reverses part of decision 33 (4), "Balance and Watch for open wherever beside"; it is Mark's call and not recommended with the open default he chose.
**Considered, not drawn.** Watch for under the Instructions in the left column: it balances Mexican Chocolate v3 (the page about 270px shorter) but leaves Olive Oil v1's right column about 1,600px short of its left, and Balance beside the table is decision 18's principle; it needs a DOM move in `RecipePage.jsx`. Keeping the side column the same height as the Sheet would mean cutting Balance, which is not this decision.
**Redrawn and checked.** The board draws the build's own markup (head on two lines, as built) at 1024 and 1366, three rows (the build, A, AB). **Conformance, board against the build with the option added** (`empty-conform.mjs`, strict, WebKit coarse: the grid's boxes in 6 panels, 288 checks, 0 off). The 17 page boards draw the build's rows (the build now) and are not redrawn for A until Mark chooses.
**App change brief (for a `/gsd-quick`, only after Mark chooses; nothing is edited under `app/`).** For A: in `app/src/styles/app.css`, inside a `@media screen and (min-width: 984px)` block (the Sheet's two columns; print is Phase 04's), set `.recipe-page { grid-template-rows: auto auto 1fr auto; }` and `.recipe-page--no-method { grid-template-rows: auto 1fr auto; }`. Test first, WebKit and Chrome at 984, 1024, 1366 and 1600 on Mexican Chocolate v3's batch: the Instructions start 49px (±1) under the table; the page height and the side column's height do not change; Olive Oil v1 and a no-Instructions recipe (Standard Base v2) read as before; below 984 nothing changes. For AB, also start Watch for closed from 984 (the fold's default in `useBelowDesktop.js` / `RecipePage.jsx`; Mark's decision 33 (4) changes).
**Not verified:** the iPad. Every reading is Playwright WebKit (coarse pointer) and system Chrome on a Mac. Whether the empty foot under A bothers Mark as the empty middle did is a look.
**Questions for Mark:** (1) A (recommended), AB, or leave as built? (2) Look at the board; the iPad's widths are 1024 and 1366. **Mark's answers (2026-10-05):** (1) A: the surplus goes to the Instructions' row (not AB, not leave). (2) Looked (marked done, no note). Build: one `/gsd-quick` with decision 48.

50. **The Batch head and a Show/Hide on the Batch section (Sid, 2026-10-05; Mark, on the iPhone: "the Batch head timestamp looks different from Tasting head timestamp, and there is no show/hide on Batch section (we need to add this capability to be consistent)"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is decided or approved).** Board `batch-head-show-hide.html`; generator `.planning/canvas-generators/batchhead.py` (from `batchhead-capture.json`, which `batchhead-probe.mjs` writes: the built page's DOM edited in the browser), with `batchhead-measure.mjs`, `batchhead-probe.mjs` (WebKit coarse, and Chrome) and `batchhead-calibrate.mjs` (the board against the build with the edit); the build is the preview server on :4173, the dist of 21:00:30, nothing started or built.
**The two heads, measured** (Olive Oil v1, its tasted batch; WebKit, coarse; distances from the log's left edge). *Batch head:* the heading "BATCH" (12px, weight 600, capitals, tracked, secondary grey) then the date "churned 2 Aug 2026" in **15px, weight 400, primary ink, with no dot**, 14px after the heading (ink 80.13 to 221.8 at 393 and 723, 68.13 to 209.8 from 724); no control. At 393 the head is 78.22px: the lead line (18.22) and, 16px under it, Correct (from 20) and Record another (from 89.35); from 724 up they stand on the date's line, 32px after it (Correct from 253.82 at 723), and the head is 44px; in the 350px log column from 1366 it is 78.22 again. *Tasting head:* a 44px fold row: "TASTING", **Show or Hide** (13px, underlined, ink), then a dot and the date in **12px, weight 400, secondary grey** (ink from 158.41 at 393 and 723, 146.41 from 724 to 1365, where the log's padding is 8px, and 133.25 at 1366), 32px after the control word; the whole row is one tap target. **So the date differs in size (15 against 12), colour (ink against grey) and the dot, and its distance from the label (14 against 32), and the Batch head has no Show or Hide.** The same at every width (724 to 1365 and 1366 included); Chrome agrees.
**A (recommended, drawn).** The Batch head becomes the fold row the Tasting head is: "BATCH", Show or Hide, a dot, "churned 2 Aug 2026" in the 12px grey (the same `fold-row` markup and rules: `FoldRow` with the date as its count), inside an h2 as Tasting's is. Correct and Record another stay in the head and follow the date (decision 34). Where they would wrap, in the log column narrower than 447px (the phone's 353 and the 350 beside the Sheet from 1366), the fold row takes the whole row, a 44px full-row control like the other folds, and they stand under it with no gap between the two 44px lines; elsewhere (723 and 724 to 1365) the fold row is as wide as its words and they follow it, so there the tap target ends at the date. **Show hides the batch's whole body** (`.batch-margin`: the measurements, the notes, Tasting with its own fold, Next time, Recorded); the head stays. **Measured in the build with the edit** (WebKit; the board agrees: 36 checks, 0 off): the head is **88px at 393 and at 1366** (78.22 now, +9.78: the date's line is now a 44px control and the 16px between the lines goes), **44px from 723 to 1365 (unchanged)**; the date starts at 139.66 (Hide) or 144.82 (Show) at 393 and 723, and at 127.66 or 132.82 from 724; Correct moves right by about 34px where it follows the date (253.82 to 287.52 at 723; 241.82 to 275.52 at 1024) and stays at 20 and 0 where it wraps. Open, the section is 9.78px taller at 393 and 1366 (556.22 to 566; 874.72 to 884.5) and unchanged from 723 to 1365 (522, 431, 416). Closed, the section is 88px at 393 and 1366 and 44px from 723 to 1365, and the page is 468px shorter at 393, 478 at 723, 372 at 1024; at 1366 the Sheet sets the page's height and nothing changes. **The wide widths change too** (it is one component): the same head at 724 to 1365, and the taller head at 1366.
**Not drawn, and why.** (1) The Correct and Record another actions inside the folded body, so the head is only the fold row: it hides Record another whenever the section is closed, and nothing else needs them to wait. (2) While a pen is open (Record another, Correct or Record a tasting) and with no batch in view ("Record a batch"): the head is the heading alone, as now; a fold there would hide the pen or has nothing to fold.
**The real choice, for Mark: the fold's default.** The folds below 1366 open closed and from 1366 open (decision 18; never stored; reset on every width crossing). **C1 (recommended): the Batch fold opens open at every width**: the page's route names the batch, Go to batch lands on it, and Show and Hide are there when the log is in the way; the phone then shows the batch as it does now. **C2: the same default as the others**, closed below 1366: History, Tasting and Batch are all closed at 393 and 1024 and the batch page opens as a head; Go to batch then has to open the fold it lands on.
**App change brief (for a `/gsd-quick`, only after Mark chooses; nothing is edited under `app/`).** In `app/src/ui/BatchRow.jsx`, where `openPen === null` and a batch is in view: the head's h2 holds `<FoldRow label="Batch" open={open} onToggle={toggle} controls="fold-batch" count={`churned ${recordDateWords(openBatch.churn.churnDate)}`} />` in place of the "Batch" text and the `.batch-row__date` span; the `.batch-margin` content goes in `<div id="fold-batch" hidden={!open}>` (all of the body, the Tasting fold included); `open` is `useFold(foldsOpen)` for C2 and a fold that starts open at every width for C1 (`useFold`'s default is the argument). With a pen open or no batch, the head is the heading alone, as now. In `app/src/styles/notebook.css`: `.notebook-log .batch-row__head { row-gap: 0; align-items: center; }`, `.notebook-log .batch-row__head-lead .region-name { margin: 0; flex: 0 1 auto; }`, `.notebook-log .batch-row__head-lead .fold-row { width: auto; }`, and, for the log column under 447px, `.notebook-log .batch-row__head-lead { flex: 0 0 100%; }`, `.region-name { flex: 1 1 auto; }` and `.fold-row { width: 100%; }`. The probe wrote the narrow rule as a container query on `.notebook-log` (`container-type: inline-size`, `@container (max-width: 446px)`); the build has no container query yet, so a `/gsd-quick` may use the log's own two widths instead (`max-width: 723.98px` and `min-width: 1366px`), with the cost that at 700 or so the actions stand under the fold row where they would fit beside it. Check: the Go to batch jump and the h2's landing focus (`#batch`, `tabIndex -1`) still land on the Batch head and show the focus ring on the fold row's button, not clip it; Go to batch opens a closed fold under C2; the `.batch-row__date` rules (`app.css`, `notebook.css`) are left unused. Test first, WebKit coarse at 393, 723, 1024 and 1366: the head heights above, the date's size and colour equal Tasting's, the dot's `::before`, the accessible name "Batch, Hide, churned 2 Aug 2026".
**Not verified:** the iPhone and iPad; every reading is Playwright WebKit (coarse pointer) and system Chrome on a Mac (Chrome with a mouse reads 68px at 393 and 1366, since Correct and Record another are 24px tall without the touch floor). The edit is a DOM edit of the built page, not a build.
**Questions for Mark:** (1) Add the fold as A (recommended)? (2) The default: C1 open at every width (recommended) or C2 closed below 1366? (3) Look at the board; the iPhone is 393, the iPad 1024 and 1366. **Mark's answers (2026-10-05):** (1) Yes, build A as drawn. (2) C1: the Batch fold is open at every width. (3) Looked (marked done, no note). Build: one `/gsd-quick` (quick 261004-uyd). **Mark's later answers (2026-10-05):** Go to batch on a fold the maker closed opens it, then lands on it (a `/gsd-quick`). The head from 487 to 723 stays 88px as built (not 44). Correct from a closed fold returning closed, and another batch reopening the fold: not asked, left as built. Mark's device check of the Batch fold on the iPhone and iPad: pass.

51. **Shared ingredients, per step: what each action does on a split ingredient (Sid, 2026-10-05; Mark, on the decide row "Which removal do you mean": "need to work through this with Sid. if an ingredient is shared across multiple steps, I want to make sure that operations on each step don't affect the other. that's not the implementation that we have today."; and his todo of 2026-10-04: "if I have 2 steps that need sugar, and I remove one of the steps, then I should not remove sugar from the recipe, only from the impacted step"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; this reopens decision 44 B; nothing here is decided or approved).** Board `per-step-shared-ingredient.html`; generators `.planning/canvas-generators/perstep.py`, `perstep-capture.mjs`, `perstep-measure.mjs`.
**What the build does today, measured** (Olive Oil v1, the Next version pen, then a saved version with Show changes; Playwright WebKit coarse at 1366 and 393 and system Chrome at 1366, the same readings in all three; the :4173 dist of 2026-10-04 21:21, nothing built or started). Olive Oil v1 is the only seeded recipe with a split ingredient (0 split lines on Mexican Chocolate v4, Mocha v3, Coconut v2, Pineapple v1, Strawberry v2.1, Underbelly Light Base v2 and Standard Base v2). Whole milk is 120 g in Step 2 and 250.4 g in Step 3, Sucrose 12 g and 64 g; the batch is 799.7 g.
1. **The remove link, on any of the four lines (decision 44 B, built in quick 261004-ox6).** It toggles one flag on the ingredient. Both lines of Whole milk strike (name, amount, share), both links read "restore", the total goes 799.7 to 429.3 g (the whole 370.4 g out) and every Balance figure reads "was". Sucrose's lines then read "17.7% in all" (9.5% before) because the batch is smaller. Pressing "restore" on either line restores both. There is no way to take one step's milk out and keep the other's.
2. **Removing a step (Step 2, or Step 3).** Nothing is struck. The step's lines move to an "Unallocated" head at the foot of the table at their full amounts: Whole milk 120 g and Sucrose 12 g after Step 2 goes (250.4 g and 64 g after Step 3), and the three gums, which only Step 2 uses. The total stays 799.7 g and no Balance figure moves. The later steps renumber (Step 3 reads "Step 2"). The removed step says "Whole milk and Sucrose are still used by step 2" (the step that still uses them, by its new number); the three gums carry "used by Gum slurry, which is removed" with "remove this row". Restoring the step moves the lines back. Saved, the version's Sheet keeps the "Unallocated" head and its lines in the reading state. So removing a step removes none of its own amounts, which is the reading of Mark's todo that the build does not meet: the 12 g of sucrose that belonged to the step is still in the recipe, under no step. (Brief § 3, decided with Mark 2026-09-07: removal "never cascades: a maker may keep step 1 and put something else in the oil".)
3. **An amount on one line.** Already per line: Step 2's milk 120 to 100 g changes that field and nothing on Step 3's line but its share (31.3% to 32.1%, as every row's share moves with the batch); the total reads 799.7 to 779.7 g. Setting a line to 0 leaves a 0 g line ("trace") in the table and in the saved version; it is the only way today to take one line out.
4. **The portion and percentage lines.** (a) After an edit the pen's portion line reads the opening total: "100 g of 370.4 g · 47.5% in all" where the lines still in total 350.4 g and 44.9% (the saved version reads 350.4 g and 44.9%); with Step 3 set to 0 it reads "120 g of 370.4 g · 67.4% in all" (the ingredient is 120 g, 21.8%). (b) Decision 44's 86.3% fix (quick ox6) covers the pen only: in Show changes, after saving a version that removed Whole milk, both lines still read "120 g of 370.4 g · 86.3% in all" and "250.4 g of 370.4 g · 86.3% in all", their amounts print plain (not struck) and their shares read 28.0% and 58.3% (the new batch without the milk; 15.0% and 31.3% of the parent's batch). (c) In Show changes a split ingredient has no per-line comparison at all (the code says so): Step 2's milk 120 to 100 g saved shows "100 g" plain on that line and nothing on the other; only the Total row's struck 799.7 says anything changed.
5. **As made, while recording.** One field per line (read from `IngredientTable.jsx`, not measured), so already per step.
**What stays whole-ingredient, whatever Mark chooses:** the batch total and every share (they are sums over the batch); the Balance figures (one coefficient set per row: the row embeds one `structuredClone` of the library entry, so milk is one set of numbers in both steps); the "estimated" tag; the row's identity, which the steps' `uses` lists name (a step "uses Whole milk", not "uses Whole milk's Step 3 line"); and the ingredient's "in all" figure, which is its total across the lines still in.
**What is drawn** (1366 and 393; Olive Oil v1; each panel is the build's own markup; the b_* panels and "Resting" are the build untouched, the others are the same page with the browser's edit of the clone, set to 0 g in the real app so the app computes the totals and shares, then the removed-row look laid on the line; the captions carry the app's numbers). *Row 1, the remove link:* resting; as built after one press; per step after one press on the Step 2 line (that line struck, "restore" on it, the Step 3 line untouched with its own "remove", total 799.7 to 679.7 g, Whole milk's Step 3 line reads "250.4 g of 250.4 g · 36.8% in all"); per step with both lines pressed (the ingredient is out, the same page as the build's one press, 429.3 g); per step with only the Step 3 line out after a restore on the Step 2 line (549.3 g). *Row 2, removing Step 2:* A as built; C the lines stay with the removed step, untouched, under a "Removed" head with the step's lead-in in the step's own place (the never-cascades rule kept, the maker removes each; 799.7 g); **B (recommended)** the lines go with the step: the five lines (120 g milk, 12 g sucrose, three gums, 133.7 g) struck under the "Removed" head, no link on them (the step's "restore" brings them back), the "used by" flags gone, the total 799.7 to 666.0 g, Whole milk in the next step "250.4 g of 250.4 g · 37.6% in all". *Row 3, an amount and Show changes:* the pen after 120 to 100 g as built and with the portion line reading the lines still in (350.4 g, 44.9%); Show changes as built and per step with the changed line carrying its struck amount and share (what a one-line row already does); and the removed-ingredient Show changes as built (86.3%, 28.0%, 58.3%) and per step (the Step 2 line struck, 120 g of 370.4 g · 46.3%, 15.0% struck; the Step 3 line 36.8%). *Rows 4 and 5:* the phone, rows 1 and 2.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides).** (1) **The remove link removes its own line (decision 44 C replaces B)**, restore likewise; the ingredient leaves the recipe when its last line goes. Mark's rule is then true for the button the maker is looking at: Step 3's milk is Step 3's. It costs a data change (below) and it is what Mark's note asks. (2) **Removing a step takes that step's lines with it (B)**, struck in the step's own place and restored with the step; a line the maker removed on its own stays out after the step is restored. This is the literal reading of the todo ("only from the impacted step") and it reverses never-cascades for lines, but not for rows or steps as wholes: the step does not remove a whole ingredient another step uses, and the `uses` flags keep their words. C is the option if Mark wants the old rule kept: it only moves "Unallocated" under its step. (3) **The portion line reads the lines still in, and Show changes strikes per line** (with the 86.3%, 28.0% and 58.3% figures corrected); the wrong figures in 4 (a) and (b) are defects whatever is chosen and can be fixed first, in their own quick.
**What it touches.** Decision 44 B (reopened, and decision 26's link position is unchanged); brief § 3's "removal never cascades" for lines (`.impeccable/surfaces/route-recipe-version.md`, which Sid amends only after Mark chooses); the 86.3% fix's scope. The data cost, plainly: today `removed` is one flag per row (a grep finds it 9 times in `domain/rows.js`, 26 in `domain/uses.js`, 13 in `diff.js`, 42 in `IngredientTable.jsx`, 26 in `RecipePage.jsx`, 51 in `Method.jsx`); per line it is a flag per portion. Not scoped by reading all of them: this is a plan, not a quick.
**App change brief (for a later `/gsd-quick` or phase plan, only after Mark chooses; nothing here edits `app/`).** (a) The model: an optional `removed` boolean on a portion (`portions[i].removed`, default false), in the pen draft and the saved row; `transfer.js` accepts it; no migration (no live data; stored rows simply lack it). `activeRows` (rows.js) returns each row with only the lines still in and drops a row with none, so `computeBalance`, `rowGrams` and the totals read lines still in with no other change; a row counts as removed when every line is out (so a one-line row behaves exactly as now). (b) The pen: `RemoveRowControl` toggles its own line (`onTogglePenLineRemoved(rowId, portionIndex)`), its accessible name unchanged ("remove Whole milk, Step 3"); the struck name, struck amount and share draw per line; `formatPortionLine` is given the lines still in and the live batch (the stale 370.4 g and 47.5% go), and a removed line reads the opening figures as decision 44's fix does. (c) Step removal under B: a line is out when its own flag is set or its step is removed (derived, not stored, so restoring the step restores exactly the lines the step took); `groupPortionsByStep` puts a removed step's lines under a "Removed" head carrying its lead-in, in the step's place, struck, with no link, and keeps "Unallocated" for a line whose step is unknown; `orphanedRows` and the step's "still used by" line are re-read against it. (d) Show changes: `diff.js` carries per-line grams and share (from, to, removed) and the table's show-changes branch strikes per line as it does for a one-line row; the 86.3%, 28.0% and 58.3% figures come out as in the board. (e) Tests: `IngredientTable.remove.test.jsx` (one flag per row becomes one per line), `IngredientTable.test.jsx`, `diff.test.js`, `rows.test.js`, `uses.test.js`, `stepNumbers.test.js`, `transfer.test.js`. Verification: the board against the build at 1366 and 393 in Playwright WebKit coarse and Chrome, then Mark's iPad and iPhone.
**Not drawn.** The reading Sheet and print when a split ingredient has one line left (it reads one line with a "250.4 g of 250.4 g" portion line; Sid would drop the portion line there, which Mark has not seen); As made while recording with a line out; 1600 fine pointer (the pen's lines are the same markup; 1366 coarse and 393 are drawn). **Not verified:** the iPad and iPhone (every reading is Playwright WebKit coarse and system Chrome on a Mac); the removed-line panels are the build's removed-row markup laid on a line by a DOM edit, not a build, so nothing here shows a per-line removal working.
**Questions for Mark:** (1) The remove link: its own line (C replaces B, recommended) or the whole ingredient (B stays)? (2) Removing a step: its lines go with it, struck (B, recommended); stay with it, untouched (C); or as built (A)? (3) Fix the wrong figures now in their own quick (4 (a) and (b)), ahead of the per-step change (recommended), or with it? (4) Does the todo "if I have 2 steps that need sugar, and I remove one of the steps, then I should not remove sugar from the recipe, only from the impacted step" read as (2) B? **Mark's answers (2026-10-05):** (1) C: each link removes its own line; decision 44 C replaces B (the 44 B build of quick ox6 is reversed by the build). (2) B: a removed step's lines go with it, struck; restoring the step brings them back. (3) With the per-step change, not in their own quick: the pen's portion line and Show changes' percentages follow the lines still in as part of that build. (4) Not asked in a row; (2) B is the reading. The build is a plan (a per-line removed flag changes rows.js, diff.js, uses.js, IngredientTable.jsx, RecipePage.jsx, the import validator and their tests), so it goes through a phase, not a quick; `.impeccable/surfaces/route-recipe-version.md` §3 ("removal never cascades") is amended after (a Run row on the list).

52. **Where the Import error list goes (Sid, 2026-10-05; Mark's answer in decision 47 (10): "Sid draws where the list goes from 724"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is decided or approved).** Board `import-errors-placement.html`; generators `.planning/canvas-generators/importerrors.py` (from `imp-capture.json`, made by `imp-capture.mjs` with the files `imp-older.json` and `imp-many.json`).
**What the build does, measured** (Olive Oil v1 at scroll 0; the error list set on the shell's own file input, as the picker would; Playwright WebKit coarse, and system Chrome for the same widths; the :4173 dist of 2026-10-04 21:21, nothing built or started). `Shell.jsx` renders the list as a `ul.shell__import-errors` inside `.shell__tools`, in the bar: `flex-basis: 100%` inside a row that does not wrap, so from 724 up it shrinks to the width left between the three controls and the bar grows to its height. Three files: *a file that is not JSON* (1 line): the bar stays 57px at 1366 (the line is 173px wide beside the controls), 64px at 724 (82px wide, wrapped to 3 lines); *an export from an older Sprinkles* (3 lines: two path lines and the plain sentence "This file is from an older version of Sprinkles and can't be imported."): the bar is **64px at 1366** (402px wide list), **79px at 1024**, **169px at 724** (120px wide list); *a wrong-shaped file* (49 lines, the validator collects every error): the bar is **754px at 1366, 1024px at 1024 and 1384px at 724**, a sticky bar 84% of a 900px window at 1366 and taller than the window at 724, over the page, with the page's first heading pushed from 133px to 830px. Chrome reads the same shapes with smaller numbers (705px at 1366, 1321px at 724: its text metrics are narrower). Below 724 the bar is not sticky: the list sits in the page's top (62px bar to 128px with 3 lines, 1133px with 49), and scrolled 400px the list is off screen, so a maker who tapped Import in More while scrolled sees nothing. **Nothing dismisses it:** the list has no control (0 buttons or links in it), stays across a route change (measured: the wordmark to Home, still there) and goes only on a successful import or a reload (from `Shell.jsx`: `setImportErrors([])` runs only on success).
**What is drawn** (1366, 724 and 393; each window a viewport, 900 high and 852 at 393, the page at its top; the A windows are the build untouched, B and C are the same page without the list in the bar and one element added, in the app's own tokens only). *A, as built:* the three files above at 1366 and 724 and 393, and 393 scrolled. **B (recommended): a panel under Import, over the page.** Fixed under the bar at its right end (the page's right gutter, 480px wide, 6px under the bar), below the Import the maker just used: the bar stays 57px and the page does not move. Plain heading "This file can't be imported" with "3 problems found" (a count, in plain words), a Close at the right (44px target), the lines under in the 12px label size, six lines high, then the list scrolls inside the panel. Measured on the board: 130px high with the 3 lines, 184px with 49 (the list 108px, scrolling). Below 724 it is fixed above the tab row at the page gutters (353px wide at 393), 148px or 184px high, so it shows wherever the page is scrolled. Close dismisses it. **C, a dialog:** the same words and list in a 520px box (8 lines, then scrolling; 172px and 262px high) centered under a scrim like the fly-out's, with Close at the foot; the maker must close it; the app has no dialog today.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): B.** The answer appears where the eye already is (under Import) and stays in view wherever the page is scrolled; it cannot grow the bar or cover the page; it needs no new modality (C blocks the page for a message the maker may only want to read once); and it fixes the phone, where the list is off screen when scrolled. Cost: the app has no panel today, so B is a small new component (one `div`, one button, one rule set, tokens only); the stacking order gains a level (above the fly-out, under the bar; see decision 53, which is answered with it: the panel is not the page notice).
**What it touches.** No approved decision is reversed. The sticky bar's 57px (decision 33) stops being broken by a long list. `shell.css` carries a comment that the tools row "stays" below 724 only so the errors have a place; that goes stale.
**App change brief (for a later `/gsd-quick`, only after Mark chooses; nothing here edits `app/`).** `Shell.jsx`: take the `ul` out of `.shell__tools` and render, after the header, `<div className="shell__import-errors" role="alert">` holding a heading, the count, a `Close` button (`tabIndex={0}`, counted by `tabindex-scan.test.js`; it calls `setImportErrors([])`; Escape closes it too, outside an open fly-out) and the `ul`; show it only while `importErrors.length > 0`; keep `key={error}` safe against repeated lines (use the index). `shell.css`: `position: fixed`; from 724 `inset-block-start: var(--app-size-header-h)`, `inset-inline-end: var(--gap-page)`, `width: 480px` (or `min(480px, 100% less the gutters)`), `margin-block-start: var(--gap-xs)`; below 724 `inset-inline: var(--gap-page)` and `inset-block-end: calc(var(--app-size-tab-h) + var(--gap-xs))`; background `--app-background`, border `--app-rule-row` solid `--app-divider`, radius `--app-radius-control`, padding `--gap-s` (`--gap-m` at the start), the title at `--app-size-meta` weight 600, the count and list at `--app-size-label` (line-height 1.5), the list `max-height: 9em` with `overflow-y: auto` and `overflow-wrap: anywhere`; Close in the rail place's own rule (`.shell__place`). A z-index token for it between `--app-z-flyout` and `--app-z-header` (above the fly-out and its scrim, under the bar; decision 53 puts the page notice below them all), and a line in `shell.test.js`'s z-order test. Remove the `.shell__import-errors` rule's `flex-basis` and the stale phone comment. Tests: `Shell.test.jsx` (the list's place, the Close, Escape), `shell.test.js` (the z order, the new rule's tokens). Check: the board against the build at 1366, 724 and 393 in WebKit coarse and Chrome with a 3-line and a 49-line file.
**Not drawn:** a rewrite of the lines themselves (they are machine paths such as "$.versions[0].rows[0].portions: expected a non-empty array, got []"; the house rule is plain words and D11 keeps technical depth available, so whether the sentence comes first, or the paths fold behind "Show details", is Mark's to say; the older-export case already has its plain sentence, second of three lines); the 1590 and up rail (the bar and the list are the same); the file picker itself (the file is set on the input directly). **Not verified:** the iPad and iPhone; B and C are drawn in a board, not built.
**Questions for Mark:** (1) A as built, B a panel under Import (recommended) or C a dialog? (2) With B, does the panel go when the maker changes page (recommended: it is stale there) or stay until Close or a good import, as the list does today? (3) Should Sid draw the lines in plain words first? **Mark's answers (2026-10-05):** (1) B: a panel under Import, over the page. (2) The panel also closes when the maker changes page. (3) Not answered (not in a row); the lines are not reworded in this build. Build: one `/gsd-quick` with decision 53, including a reset of the stray border on `button.shell__place` so the Close button does not draw it (decision 54's finding).

53. **How the page notice stacks with the open fly-out (Sid, 2026-10-05; Mark's answer in decision 47 (10): the decision 33 brief's order, scrim, fly-out, notice, header, "is open again for the notice"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is decided or approved).** Board `notice-over-flyout.html`; generators `.planning/canvas-generators/noticestack.py` (from `notice-capture.json`, made by `notice-capture.mjs`).
**What the build does, measured** (Olive Oil v1 saved as a new version, which raises "Version saved." as the page notice, then the menu button pressed; Playwright WebKit coarse at 724, 1024, 1366 and 1589 (the same at all four), and system Chrome for the same widths (the notice 110.5 x 29px there, the same overlap); the :4173 dist of 2026-10-04 21:21, nothing built or started). The tokens are scrim 4, fly-out 5, notice 10, bar 11 (`tokens.css`; `shell.test.js` "the z order" pins literal page z-indexes < scrim < fly-out < notice < bar). `.page-status` is `position: absolute` in a zero-height anchor at the top of the page, 48px from the left, 6px under the bar's foot: so at the top of the page it sits at x 48, y 63, "Version saved." 114 x 30px, and the app's own "Tasting removed. You can restore it." 242.8 x 30px. The open fly-out is fixed from y 57 at x 0 to 224 wide. They overlap: the notice paints over **Home**, the panel's first place (63 to 107 high): 114 x 30px of it for the short notice (the whole notice, inside the panel) and 155 x 30px for the long one (which ends 67px past the panel's edge), hiding Home's icon and word and the focus ring the build puts on Home as the panel opens. It paints over the scrim too, so the page under the scrim is dimmed and the notice is bright. The main is `inert` while the panel is open, so a tap on the covered Home reaches Home (measured: the element at the notice's centre is the Home link). The notice clears after 5 seconds, so the overlap exists only if the menu is opened within 5 seconds of a notice and the page is at its top: scrolled 600px the notice has scrolled off (y -486 in WebKit, -87 in Chrome) and nothing overlaps. Below 724 there is no fly-out. **Side finding:** the notice scrolls away with the page (it is not fixed), so a notice raised below the page's top is not seen at all; not part of this question.
**What is drawn** (1366 and 724; each window the top 360px; the fly-out is the build's own nav placed by hand, with the scrim, the menu pressed and Home focused; no transform wraps the page, so z-index decides the stack as it does in the app). Rows A, B and C, each with "Version saved." at 1366, the longer notice at 1366 and at 724. *A, as built:* the notice over Home and the scrim. **B (recommended):** the notice is part of the page, so it sits under the scrim and under the panel (notice token 10 to 3, the order notice < scrim < fly-out < bar): the short notice is wholly under the panel (nothing shows, as the panel opens); the long one shows its last 67px dimmed beside the panel; Home and its ring are clear. *C:* opening the menu clears the notice (nothing to stack; its text is a live region and was announced when it came).
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): B.** One token value, no behaviour: the notice belongs to the page it is about, so under the scrim like everything else on the page ("the page beneath is unchanged", decision 33), and the panel is the only thing the maker is looking at. It also removes the one case where an overlay paints over the dimmed page undimmed. A reverses the brief's order for good reason only if Mark wants the notice always visible; C adds a behaviour (the menu clears a message) for the same screen as B. **Not drawn:** the notice moved beside the panel while it is open (a jump from x 48 to 272, which breaks "nothing moves"); the notice pinned under the bar (it would fix the scrolled-away side finding, and it is a different decision).
**What it touches.** The decision 33 brief's stacking, for the notice only (Mark said it is open again): `tokens.css` `--app-z-notice` and its comments (the scrim's comment, "above every z-index the page itself uses", gains the notice), `shell.test.js` "the z order" (literals < notice < scrim < fly-out < bar). No approved board changes. It interacts with decision 52: the Import error panel must sit above the fly-out and under the bar, so its token goes between `--app-z-flyout` and `--app-z-header`.
**App change brief (for a later `/gsd-quick`, only after Mark chooses B; nothing here edits `app/`).** `tokens.css`: `--app-z-notice: 3` with its comment ("the page notice, as built (.page-status); a layer of the page, so under the scrim and the fly-out; above the page's own 1 and 2"); the `--app-z-scrim` and `--app-z-header` comments say where the notice now stands. `shell.test.js`: the z-order test asserts `max(literals) < notice < scrim < flyout < header`. Nothing in `shell.css`, `app.css` or `Shell.jsx`. Check, in WebKit coarse at 724 and 1366 with the real flow (save a version, press the menu within 5 seconds): Home and its focus ring are clear; the notice is gone or dimmed beside the panel; closing the panel within the 5 seconds shows the notice again, bright.
**Not verified:** the iPad and iPhone; B and C are drawn in a board, not built (B's dimmed stack comes from changing `.page-status`'s z-index in the board's copy of the stylesheet).
**Questions for Mark:** (1) A as built, B the notice under the scrim and panel (recommended), or C the menu clears the notice? **Mark's answers (2026-10-05):** (1) B: the notice goes under the scrim and the panel (the notice token from 10 to 3; the Import error panel sits between the fly-out and the bar). Build: one `/gsd-quick` with decision 52.

54. **What More's items look like on the iPhone (Sid, 2026-10-05; Mark's radius check, decision 47 (8): "More's items on iPhone don't look right: Import and Export look like very small buttons with each having a dark rounded rectangle border"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is decided or approved).** Board `more-items-iphone.html`; generators `.planning/canvas-generators/more.py` (from `more-capture.json`, made by `more-capture.mjs`; the option rules are `more-candidates.css`, which `more-candidates.mjs` also injects into the build; `more-board-measure.mjs` measures the board), readings by `more-measure.mjs` (and `more-hover.mjs`, `more-hover-tools.mjs`). The More tab's clipped focus ring in the same check was fixed by quick 261004-uo6 and is not redrawn.
**What each item is, measured** (393 x 852, Playwright WebKit coarse and system Chrome; More tapped open; Olive Oil v1, and `/ingredients` so Ingredients is the current place; the :4173 dist, modified 2026-10-04 22:10:31, which carries quick ubg and not quick uo6 (the tab row's inset ring rule is absent from its CSS), nothing built or started; the list's own rules are the same in the source and the dist). The panel is `details.shell__more[open] > ul`: white, 1px solid #d6dad7, 12px padding, no radius, shrunk to its widest item, sitting on the tab row at the window's right edge: **89.5 x 275px** in WebKit (85.6 x 270 in Chrome), 93.2 wide on Ingredients because the current place is weight 600. *Ingredients, Kitchen, Search* are `a.shell__place` links (NavLink; they navigate and the panel closes): 63.5 x 49, no border, transparent, 10px radius, an icon over a 12px word with no side padding (the tab row's rule `.shell__tabs .shell__place` reaches the list), in the destination's text colour (green, indigo) and ink for Search; the current one sits on the subtle surface #f3f4f2 in weight 600, 67.2 x 49 with the word touching the surface's left and right edges (0px each side). *Import and Export* are `button.shell__place`, type button: Import clicks the shell's one hidden file input (`input[type=file]`, display none, tabindex -1, aria-hidden, shared with the header's Import), Export builds a Blob and downloads it. Their computed style: **1px solid rgb(20,20,20) border**, transparent fill, the same 10px radius, 12px, and **39.1 x 51 and 38.9 x 51** (37.1 x 50 and 37.3 x 50 in Chrome): a button does not stretch to its list item the way a link does, so each is as wide as its word, **under the 44px touch minimum** (`--touch-min`). Under a pointer's hover the border goes to 2px and the box to 41.1 x 53 (the Sheet's `button:hover`), and a tap leaves hover on iOS until the next tap elsewhere (not reproduced here). Focus (the app's `.shell__place:focus`, script focus): 2px solid ink, offset 2px, following the 10px radius; the ring stays inside the panel (the Import ring's right edge is at 359.6, the panel's at 393), which is why quick uo6 left the list's outset ring alone.
**Why they differ (read in `app.css` and `shell.css`, then confirmed by the numbers above).** The Sheet's own `button` rule is global (`app.css` 52: `appearance: none; border: var(--rule-ink-field) solid var(--sheet-ink); border-radius: 0; background: none; font-size ...`, and `button:hover` thickens the border to 2px). The header's tools carry `.shell__tools button { appearance: none; border: none; background: none; color: inherit; cursor: pointer }` (`shell.css` 186), so the same two buttons read borderless there (0px none, 108.4 x 44, 14px, padding 12px 20px at 724 and 1366); More's list has no such reset. Decision 38's 10px radius sits on every `.shell__place`, which is what turns the stray Sheet border into "a dark rounded rectangle". Mark's words match the numbers: very small (39px wide), boxed (1px ink on 10px), dark (ink).
**What the documents say.** D-16 (`03.4-CONTEXT.md`): More holds Ingredients, Kitchen, Search, Import and Export, and nothing on how its items look; board 171 draws More closed. Plan 03.4-06 decision 5: the open panel "carries a border and the App background, and no radius ... the minimum that makes it legible", so the panel and its items were never drawn; the 03.4 UI review's finding 4 (P3: "More panel 85.6px wide, Import/Export 50px items: cosmetic; Mark's call") flagged these numbers and no answer is recorded. Decision 38 (Mark: B) and its brief put the 10px radius on every place and the focus ring on that shape; nothing gives a place a border, in the rail, the tab row or the tools. DESIGN.md "Shapes": "The Sheet's square-edge and browser-chrome treatments do not bind the app interface", and its catalogue says app components need their own approved states: the Sheet's hairline button is the wrong context here. The surface briefs under `.impeccable/surfaces` do not mention More's panel. The plain-words rule holds as built: five short sentence-case words (Ingredients, Kitchen, Search, Import, Export), no change. The shared size rule: the panel reads these controls at the tab row's 12px (`--app-size-label`), the header at the rail's 14px (`--app-size-meta`): the same three controls (Search, Import, Export) at two sizes, which no decision names.
**What is drawn** (`more-items-iphone.html`, 1339 x 3456; each window the app's own 393 shell markup with More open, the app's stylesheets resolved at 393, the tab row and panel at the window's foot; three states per row: Notebook page, On Ingredients (its place current), Import focused with the app's own ring put on by hand). **A, as built:** the numbers above. **B, tiles, the border gone:** the tools row's own button reset also on More's buttons, a button as wide as its list item, and 6px side padding (`--gap-xs`) so the current place's surface has room around the word: every item 75.5 x 49, panel **101.5 x 271**; on Ingredients 79.2 x 49 with the word 6px from each edge; hover keeps the border at 0. **C, rows (recommended):** the same reset, and the list's items take the rail place's own values (icon beside the 14px word, 12px and 20px padding) instead of the tab row's: every item **138.4 x 44**, panel **164.4 x 246** (168.8 on Ingredients, the surface 142.8 x 44), which is how Search, Import and Export already read in the header from 724. **D, rows with a hairline:** C with the rail's own divider (`.shell__divider`, 6px and 20px margins) between Search and Import: panel **164.4 x 259**. Chrome reads B 97.6 x 266 (items 71.6 x 48), C 161.5 x 246 and D 161.5 x 259, the same shapes. The board's panel sizes are the build's with each option's rules injected (`more-candidates.mjs`): identical in WebKit.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): C.** The three controls are one kind of thing in this app, a place, and the header already draws them as 44px rows with the word at 14px; below 724 they move into More and should not change shape. C removes both defects (the stray border and the 39px target) and the tile's side-padding problem together, every item is one full-width 44px target, and the 14px word is the app's label size for a place. B is the smallest change and the panel stays the narrow strip Mark has seen (89 against 164px), but its labels stay at 12px and Search, Import and Export stay a different size from the header. D adds a grouping (places go somewhere, Import and Export do something); the header's tools row has no hairline there and the rail only separates its own groups, so I leave it out. The cost of C: the panel is 75px wider and covers more of the page while it is open (it is shorter, 246 against 275).
**What it touches.** No approved decision is reversed: decision 38's radius and ring are unchanged. DESIGN.md is not edited; if Mark chooses, one line for an `/impeccable document` pass: a place that is a button carries no border, in the rail, the tools and More. The tab row's own tiles are not touched.
**App change brief (for a later `/gsd-quick`, only after Mark chooses C; nothing here edits `app/`).** (1) `shell.css`, top level: the reset on `button.shell__place` (appearance none, border none, background none, color inherit, cursor pointer) so a place that is a button is reset by what it is, wherever it sits, and the `.shell__tools button` rule goes (decision 52's B draws its Close as `.shell__place` with the reset in the board's own CSS; the brief for it says only "in the rail place's own rule", which today would draw this same border on Close: this rule covers it). (2) `shell.css`, phone block: `.shell__more li > button { width: 100% }` and `.shell__tabs .shell__more li .shell__place { flex-direction: row; justify-content: flex-start; gap: var(--gap-xs); padding: var(--gap-s) var(--gap-m); font-size: var(--app-size-meta); text-align: start; width: 100% }` (the rail place's own values; the selectors start with `.shell__tabs` and `.shell__more`, which the media allowlist in `shell.test.js` admits). (3) `shell.test.js`: pin the reset on `button.shell__place` and the More list's row rule (the tokens, the 100% width). `Shell.jsx` does not change for C. For D only: an `<li className="shell__more-sep" aria-hidden="true"><hr className="shell__divider" /></li>` before Import's item, and `.shell__more li.shell__more-sep { min-height: 0 }` in the phone block (the `.shell__more li` touch rule would make it 44px); no new link or button, so `tabindex-scan.test.js` is untouched. For B instead: the reset above, `width: 100%` on `.shell__more li > button`, and `.shell__tabs .shell__more li .shell__place { padding-inline: var(--gap-xs) }`. The rules are the board's own, in `more-candidates.css`. Check: the board against the build at 393 in WebKit coarse and Chrome, three states (rest, on Ingredients, Import focused), hover in Chrome (border 0), a tap on each item (the panel closes; Import opens the picker, one input), then the iPhone.
**Found while measuring, not drawn.** (1) At 1366 (and from 724) hovering the header's Import or Export with a pointer shrinks it from 108.4 x 44 to 80.4 x 32 (79 x 32 in Chrome), because the Sheet's `button:hover` sets `padding: var(--gap-xs)` at specificity (0,1,1) and `.shell__place`'s 12px and 20px is (0,1,0); More's items do not shrink (the tab row's selector outranks it). It shows on the iPad with a mouse or trackpad. A reset on `button.shell__place` that also sets the padding on hover would fix it in the same quick; Mark has not named it. So B's tiles plus the hairline between Search (places) and Import (actions), a combination not drawn; Sid draws it, then a `/gsd-quick`. Tiles 75.5 x 49 each (79.2 x 49 on Ingredients, the word 6px from the surface's left and right edges); Import and Export 75.5 x 49, border 0, and under hover the border stays 0 and the box does not move (as built they go to 2px and 41.1 x 53). The rule's line (75.5 x 1 at rest, 79.2 x 1 on Ingredients) is at 6px under Kitchen's tile and 6px over Search's (tiles at y 526, 575, rule at 630, Search 637, Import 686, Export 735 in a 852 window). With Import focused, the ring (2px, offset 2px) stays 8px inside the panel's left and right borders and reaches 4px into Search's tile, as the ring does between any two tiles with no gap (-4px, WebKit and Chrome); with Search focused the ring clears the rule by 2px and is 8px inside both borders. **Search** (an `a.shell__place`, measured at rest, hover and focus beside Import and Ingredients): the border reset and the tile rules already cover it and nothing differs. At rest it is 75.5 x 49 (79.2 on Ingredients), border 0, padding 6px, radius 10px, 12px type, no underline, the pointer cursor, ink (#141414; the `:where(a:visited)` rule sets the same ink, so a visited Search looks the same), identical to Import's tile in every property but the tag; under hover nothing moves or changes (no border, no background, no underline, 75.5 x 49 before and after), and the 2px ring (offset 2px, ink) is the same as Import's; against Ingredients, the other link, only the colour differs (Ingredients carries its place colour). A tap on Search goes to /search and closes More (WebKit and Chrome). On the board the panel matches the build's 101.5 x 284 and 105.2 x 284. 56. **As made while recording a batch on a version with a line out (Sid, 2026-10-05; Mark's List row per-step-as-made-line-out, his note: "ask Sid to draw this and recommend the solution/approach"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is decided or approved).** Board `as-made-line-out.html`; generators `.planning/canvas-generators/asmade.py`, `asmade-capture.mjs`, `asmade-measure.mjs`, `asmade-conform.mjs`.
**What the build does, measured** (Olive Oil v1, Next version, press remove on Whole milk's Step 2 line, save as v2, then Record a batch, Correct after saving; real clicks and fills on app/dist served on a throwaway 127.0.0.1 port, Playwright WebKit coarse and system Chrome, 1366 and 393, the same in all four; the dist of 09:04 on 2026-10-05, which carries quick 261005-cgb). (1) The reading Sheet does not draw a line that is out (13 lines, none struck, Total 679.7 g) and recording shares that table with the as-made column added: 13 fields, one per line still in, plan 679.7 g, none for Whole milk's Step 2 line. (2) A value stays on its own line by stored position: typing 245 in Whole milk, 12.5 and 63 in Sucrose stores Whole milk [null, 245] and Sucrose [12.5, 63]; the as-made total reads 673.8 g against plan 679.7 g; Correct reopens the same fields with the same values; the saved batch reads "as made 245 g" on the one Whole milk line. (3) Whole milk's one field is named "Whole milk, as made, grams, portion 2" (a screen reader says portion 2; nothing on screen says portion, and there is no portion 1). (4) Removing a step is the same rule on five lines: Gum slurry out leaves 9 fields, plan 666.0 g, and Whole milk's and Sucrose's one field are each named portion 2. A whole ingredient out (both Whole milk lines) leaves 12 fields and no Whole milk row, as before 03.6. (5) A step has its own "Skipped" box and a "Done differently" line while recording (named "Step 2, skipped", "Step 2, done differently").
**What does not hold together, plainly.** (a) Quick 261005-cgb drops the portion line on the reading Sheet when one line is left but deliberately left recording alone until this is answered: in the same version the Sheet reads "Whole milk 250.4 g" and the recording table reads "250.4 g of 250.4 g · 36.8% in all" under it. (b) The field's accessible name counts the stored position, not what is drawn, so a lone line is "portion 2"; it is not a rare case, because removing any step does it to every ingredient the step shared. (c) A cannot hold one thing: a maker who adds a removed line back while churning has no field for it, so the as-made total is short by that amount and nothing on the page says so; the only place for it is the step's "Done differently" line. (d) Step 2's own text in v2 still reads "Whisk into the milk" and "At 120 g this is a thin layer in the pan": the text is authored and is not tied to the lines (not drawn, not a defect of this decision).
**Drawn** (1366 and 393; each panel is the build's own markup, the real page after real clicks; the B panels are the same page after a browser edit of the clone, the build's removed look, a struck name and a struck amount and no share, over the build's own as-made field; nothing is a build). *Row 1, Whole milk's Step 2 line out:* the reading Sheet; A as built; A recommended, with 245, 12.5 and 63 typed; B resting; B with 120 typed on the struck line. *Row 2, Gum slurry out:* v1 with nothing out (14 fields, 799.7 g); A as built (9); A recommended (9); B (14, the five lines under a Removed head, struck). *Rows 3 and 4:* the phone. Numbers: A is 13 fields at 936.6 px from the heading to the Total at 1366 (1223.7 at 393); B is 14 fields, 57.33 px taller at 1366 (struck row 57.33) and 81 px taller at 393; for Gum slurry B adds five struck rows: 993.96 against 672.74 at 1366 and 1304.7 against 866.1 at 393. B's as-made total with 120 typed on the struck line is 793.8 g (673.8 + 120), a figure the app does not compute: the build's rule (a written value counts, a blank takes the plan) applied by hand, the struck line's plan being 0. Conformance: board against build, 1296 checks, 0 off (WebKit and Chrome, 1366 and 393; row heights, tops, field boxes, labels, totals within 1px; the recommended A's panels compare geometry and totals only, since their labels are the proposed change).
**Options.** A (as built): a line that is out has no row and no field in recording, like the reading Sheet and print. A recommended: A, and recording reads like the Sheet when one line is drawn (no portion line, the field named without "portion N"). B: the line stays in recording, struck, with its own field; for one rule it must also hold for a removed step (five more fields for Gum slurry) and for a whole ingredient out; it needs a word to say what a field beside a struck line asks, which is new vocabulary; and Phase 04's printed sheet would need a writing space for lines that are not on the reading Sheet.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): A, recommended.** (1) The maker writes on the printed Sheet and transcribes it; the Sheet has no row for a line that is out, so there is nothing to transcribe. B would draw rows that exist nowhere else. (2) One table: reading, recording and print stay one set of rows with the as-made column added, which is the rule Mark confirmed for the pen and for Show changes. (3) B is not one rule unless it covers steps and whole ingredients, and that puts up to five empty fields in every recording of a version with a removed step (measured above). (4) The one thing A cannot hold, adding a line back, has the step's "Done differently" line. (5) A needs no data change: the as-made arrays already align to the stored lines. If Mark wants the as-made total to count a line added back, B is the way and this board is the drawing of it; the cost is above.
**What stays as built, whichever Mark chooses:** each typed value stays on its own line by stored position (Whole milk [null, 245]); the as-made total is taken over the lines drawn (673.8 g); a whole ingredient out has no row; the saved batch reads "as made 245 g" on the line that remains; Correct reopens the same fields.
**App change brief (for a later `/gsd-quick`, only after Mark chooses A recommended; nothing here edits `app/`).** In `IngredientTable.jsx`, `renderReadingEntry`: take the lines drawn for `isSplit` in recording as the reading Sheet already does (quick 261005-cgb left `(mode === 'recording' ? row : liveRow)` for this answer, so recording reads `liveRow` too), and in `AsMadeCell` name the field "portion N" only when the row draws two or more lines, N counting the lines drawn; the value stays written at the stored index (`portion.index ?? i`), so nothing stored changes. Tests: recording and Correct on a row with one line left (no portion line, the field's name, the value at the stored index), and a row with two of three lines left. If B is chosen instead it is a plan, not a quick (the recording table, `asMadeTotals` counting out lines at plan 0, Correct, the saved batch's reading of a struck line, print, and a word for the field); not scoped.
**Not drawn.** 1600 with a fine pointer (the same markup; 1366 coarse and 393 are drawn). **Not verified:** the iPad and iPhone (every reading is Playwright WebKit coarse and system Chrome on a Mac; Chrome reads the table 0.97 px narrower than WebKit). **Question for Mark:** A recommended, A as built, or B? **Mark's answer (2026-10-05, Mark's List row look-as-made-line-out-board): "A recommended".** Decided: recording reads like the Sheet when one line is drawn; B is not built. The app change is the brief above, run as a `/gsd-quick`.

57. **Where the sentence goes when a save is blocked on an ingredient line (Sid, 2026-10-05; Mark's List row blocked-row-sentence-not-shown, his answer "have Sid draw where the sentence goes"; drawn, awaiting Mark's look; nothing here is decided or approved).** Board `blocked-row-sentence.html`; generators `.planning/canvas-generators/blocked.py`, `blocked-capture.mjs`, `blocked-conform.mjs`.
**What the build does, measured** (Olive Oil v1, Next version, "v2" typed in Version name, then Whole milk's Step 3 amount cleared, or Heavy cream's, or "4o" typed, then Save as a new version; real clicks and fills on a copy of app/dist (the dist of 09:25 on 2026-10-05, after quick 261005-cm6) on a throwaway 127.0.0.1 port; Playwright WebKit coarse and system Chrome, 1366 and 393). (1) The page scrolls to the blocked line and the cursor lands in its field (WebKit: scrolled 877px for Whole milk's Step 3 line and 949px for Heavy cream at 1366, 1192 and 1268px at 393; Chrome within 5px of that). The line goes bold with a 1px outline, the field gets a 2px ring, and typing a character clears both. (2) No sentence is printed for the line, at either width, in either engine: no `.field-error` in the page. (3) Save is the only save control and it sits in the ceremony at the top of the page: after the scroll it is 508px (Whole milk) and 580px (Heavy cream) above the top of the view at 1366, and 588px and 664px at 393 (Chrome 506, 578, 588, 663). The ceremony's status line is empty, and the field has no `aria-invalid` and no `aria-describedby`, so a screen reader hears nothing for a line; for the version line the build prints the sentence beneath the field and "Check the version. Your changes have been kept." above the buttons, and the page does not scroll. (4) The other sentence, "Whole milk's amount is not a number" ("4o" typed), behaves the same.
**What does not hold together, plainly.** (a) The brief says the opposite of the build: `.impeccable/surfaces/route-recipe-version.md` (Fields): "The blocked-save sentence renders at the ceremony and the offending row is marked in the table by the same weight-and-outline the trace uses, so the message and its cause are both on screen." The build marks the line and prints nothing at the ceremony; and printed at the ceremony the sentence would not be on screen with its cause: the cursor lands 508 to 664px from it. (b) Olive Oil v1 has two Whole milk lines (Step 2 and Step 3). The sentence names the ingredient, not the line, and says "remove it": printed away from the line, it does not say which Whole milk. (c) Quick 261005-cm6 built the per-line sentence and its focus, and left the print for this answer; nothing in the page says why Save did nothing except the outline, which a person looking away from the line does not see.
**Drawn** (1366 and 393; each panel is the build's own markup, the real page after real clicks; the sentence panels are the same page after a browser edit of the clone, laid where the option puts it; nothing is a build). *Built, three panels:* the line after Save; the ceremony at that moment; Version name blank, for comparison. *Options, four panels:* A, in the line (Whole milk's Step 3, Heavy cream, "4o"); B, at the ceremony. Conformance, board against build in WebKit and Chrome, 1366 and 393: 1024 checks, 0 off (row tops and heights, field boxes, name cells and the ceremony's height, status line and button tops within 1px in WebKit and 2px in Chrome).
**Options.** *As built:* the outline and the cursor only. *A, the sentence in the line (recommended):* a `.field-error` span last in the line's own name cell, under the portion line when there is one: 12px, bold because the marked line is bold, in the face the version line's sentence already has. The field names it (`aria-describedby`) and is `aria-invalid`, so a screen reader reads it as focus lands. Row heights, WebKit: Whole milk's Step 3 line 75.3 to 88.1px at 1366 (+12.8, one line in a 382px name cell) and 95.2 to 126.0px at 393 (+30.8, two lines in 219px); Heavy cream 75.3 to 75.3px at 1366 (the sentence fits the row's own spare space) and 81.0 to 111.6px at 393 (+30.6); "4o": +12.8 and +30.8. Chrome: +12.8 at 1366; at 393 +25.2 (Whole milk, 81.0 to 106.2px), +30.8 (Heavy cream), +10.8 ("4o", one line), because Chrome wraps the name cell 5.6px wider. *B, at the ceremony:* the sentence in the ceremony's status line above the buttons, the slot the version line already uses (336 to 355.5px, +19.5; WebKit; Chrome 333 to 352.5). It is printed, and out of view.
**Recommendation (Sid; Mark decides): A.** (1) The cursor is already in that line, so the sentence is where the eye is; B puts it 508 to 664px away from the cause. (2) The line says which Whole milk: the sentence sits under the line it names, which removes (b). (3) It uses a sentence, a face and an id the version line already has; no new word, size or colour. (4) It costs one line (12.8px) at 1366 and two at 393, and none on a one-line row at 1366. (5) It goes when the amount is typed, with the outline, as the build clears both today. A is not the brief's sentence ("renders at the ceremony"): if Mark chooses A, the Fields paragraph of the brief is amended to say the line (Sid does that when he chooses; not done here). The status line at the ceremony stays empty for a line, as built, so a blocked line is not said twice; the version line's pair of sentences is not a precedent for lines, since the version field is on screen when its sentence is.
**What stays as built, whichever Mark chooses:** the wording ("Whole milk needs an amount, or remove it", "Whole milk's amount is not a number"; `blockedSaveMessage`); the first blocked line wins; the page scrolls to it and the cursor lands in its field; the bold line, the 1px outline and the 2px ring; the version line's sentence under its field and its status above the buttons; the figures keep reading the last good value; the struck old amount under an emptied field; and nothing is saved.

No CSS: `.field-error` is the version line's rule; the marked line's weight is inherited. Tests: a blocked save prints the sentence in the blocked line's cell and nowhere else (blank on a split line, blank on a one-line row, "4o"); the field is `aria-invalid` and described by it; typing clears it; the version line's own sentence is unchanged. If Mark chooses B instead: the status line takes the sentence in `buildPenFields`, a one-line change, and the brief stays as it is.

## How the boards are made

Every board is generated, never hand-edited — `.planning/canvas-generators/gen.py` makes all but the long-history board, which `longhist.py` makes on top of gen.py. Each file here is the canvas `.dc.html` with the canvas support script removed and the app stylesheet asset inlined in a style element; the Caveat @font-face points at `../../../app/public/fonts/caveat-regular.woff2` and a Google Fonts Caveat link also remains; the `<x-dc>` and `<helmet>` wrappers and the `text/x-dc` script stay and are inert in a plain browser; each root element carries the board's fixed width and height; each rung carries only the narrow media bodies its layout gets, unwrapped from `phone-forced.css` (the tab row below 984, the one-column Sheet below 984, the phone forms below 724, the touch floor only on the 393 board, since touch follows the pointer), and the 984 and 1024 boards force the two-column Sheet so a narrow viewer window cannot collapse them.

**The wide boards from 2026-10-03 (decision 33) come from `final.py`, not `gen.py`:** `final.py` runs after `gen.py` and overwrites `R35C_744`, `R35C_834`, `R35C_983`, `R35C_984`, `R35C_1024`, `R35C_1366`, `R35C_Batch`, `R35C_1920`, `R35C_NoBatch`, `R35C_Pen`, `R35C_LongHistory`, `R35C_GoToBatchWide`, `R35C_1366Sticky` and `R35C_984Sticky` with pages built from `final-capture.json` (the built app's shell markup, `final-capture.mjs`), the app's own stylesheets resolved at the window and the final design's rules (`nav-candidates.css`, `ingredient-options.css`); `final-calibrate.mjs` sizes the windows and writes the captions' numbers (`final-heights.json`, `final-board-measure.json`), and `snapshot.py` takes its inlined stylesheet from `393-batch.html`, since `1600-batch.html` no longer carries it. `gen.py` still writes the old hand-drawn versions of those keys, which are not used. To change a board: edit the generator (re-reading the as-built board, canvas.json and the stylesheet asset from the canvas first, per gen.py's SRC path), regenerate, grep the outputs for every approved change, publish, then re-snapshot here. Never patch a file in this folder.

**Since 2026-10-04 (decisions 45 and 46):** the boards read the build as it stands. `redraw.sh` retakes the captures (`final-capture.mjs`, `phone-capture.mjs`, `breaks-capture.mjs`, all from the preview server on :4173) and runs `pen.py`, `breaks.sh` (the print break board's three passes) and `phone.py`; `redraw-all.sh` does that, snapshots into a scratch folder, calibrates the page heights (`final-calibrate.mjs`, `phone-measure.mjs`) and runs the checks (`redraw-conform.mjs`, `redraw-conform-open.mjs`; `UNBUILT=d3,jump,p3,dots` names what a board may still draw ahead of the build). `gen.py` is imported by every generator and rewrites its own hand-drawn boards each time, so the order of the runs matters: `pen.py`, then `breaks.py`, then `phone.py` last. `final.py` and `phone.py` add D3, P3, JUMP and the dots' block only where `built_d3`, `built_p3`, `built_jump` and `built_dots` find the app's own CSS lacking them. `snapshot.py` takes the board keys to write: pass the changed ones.

**Canvas pages split 2026-10-06 (Sid; Mark: "the Recipe Route page represent what is selected and built and the concepts pushed to another page"; canvas version 325).** Page "Recipe route 03.5" now holds 38 boards, the ones Mark chose that the app builds, or the build's own capture (`redraw.sh`); page "Recipe route 03.5 concepts" holds the other 47, with their title notes and side notes (46 notes moved; 22 stayed). Nothing was deleted, regenerated or renamed: each board kept its key, title, size and content, and only its page and place changed (index only, no board file sent; comments are anchored to the boards, so they went with them). ## What is authority here

This sketch is the single authority for 03.5 agents — planner, checker, executors and UAT read the boards, including their CSS, not a paraphrase. Conformance is judged side-by-side in a browser that has visited the routes, never from prose or a test count.

- The Recipe Book board is a reference only (the form and its route are not built in 03.5), and its derived facts strip and footnote predate the 2026-09-24 Yield and time decision, which governs.
- Mark and the 03.5 discussion, 2026-09-24: the Recipe Book board as drawn exposed the version structure (version selection, Next version and the History rail in the band, as in the Notebook), which the discussion settled is wrong. The Recipe Book shows one selected version only — no version selection, no Next version and no History — and its band carries the Recipe name (product brief; D18; 05-domain-and-language: Add to Recipe Book selects a trusted version without moving or duplicating history). This principle is settled now; the reference board itself is redrawn in the phase that builds the Recipe Book form, and the route stays reserved, not built, until then.
- Versions 3–8 on the long-history board are illustrative, not seed content.
- The not-chosen pen alternatives (focused-only controls and always-shown controls), layouts A and B, and the ingredient-table alternatives stay on the canvas only and are not live alternatives.

## How to view

```
python3 -m http.server 8011   # run from the repo root
```

then open `http://localhost:8011/.planning/sketches/011-recipe-route-c/index.html`. Serving from the repo root is what makes the font path resolve; a server rooted at `.planning/sketches/` (the 8077 habit from sketch 009) cannot reach it. For the iPad or iPhone, use the Mac's LAN address and hard-reload, since the server sends no cache headers.
